Fails if: one does. | compat | agreed |

### CRP: crops, tags and adapters (1.1)

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-CRP-01 | The default tag `cobblemon_dynamics:cultivator_targets` MUST hold `#minecraft:crops` and each growable family of the adapter table of ADR-0015 that `#minecraft:crops` lacks, with `"required": false` on each entry of a mod other than Minecraft and Cobblemon. | MUST | R73, R75, RES8, ADR 0015 | Ruling 5 of R73: "The defaults cover vanilla crops plus COHO's crop mods". `#minecraft:crops` brings every mod that tags its crops, and the explicit entries add the crops that no mod tags, such as Cobblemon's mints and berry trees and the Aether's blueberry bushes. An entry with `required` false lets the tag load without its mod. `#minecraft:bee_growables` stays out, because Cobblemon puts apricorns and saccharine leaves in it (RES8). | Unit: `FarmingTagFilesTest`: the file holds `#minecraft:crops` and each growable family of the adapter table, every entry outside `minecraft` and `cobblemon` has `required` false, and the file names no `#minecraft:bee_growables`. Fails if: a family is missing, an entry of another mod is required, or the tag names `#minecraft:bee_growables`. | build | agreed |
| CD-CRP-02 | The default tag `cobblemon_dynamics:harvester_targets` MUST hold `#minecraft:crops` and each harvestable family of the adapter table of ADR-0015 that `#minecraft:crops` lacks, with `"required": false` on each entry of a mod other than Minecraft and Cobblemon. | MUST | R73, RES8, ADR 0015 | As CD-CRP-01, for the Harvester: the berry trees, bushes, attached stems, cave vines, cocoa, nether wart and Pam's fruit come from explicit entries. | Unit: `FarmingTagFilesTest`, as for CD-CRP-01, with the harvestable families. Fails if: a family is missing, or an entry of another mod is required. | build | agreed |
| CD-CRP-03 | The farming handlers MUST read each crop through the first crop adapter that claims its block, in this order: the adapter tags `#cobblemon_dynamics:harvest/never`, `harvest/pick_to_age_0`, `harvest/pick_to_age_1`, `harvest/upper_half` and `harvest/break_and_replant`, then the adapters of the classes `BerryBlock`, `CaveVines`, `AttachedStemBlock` and `CropBlock`, and, for growth only, `BonemealableBlock`. | MUST | R73, RES8, ADR 0015 | A pack gives a crop of another mod to an adapter with a tag, as it gives targets to a role with a tag (ruling 5). The tags come first because some crops need another harvest than their vanilla class gives: Farmer's Delight's tomatoes and Pam's fruit are `CropBlock`s that a pick harvests, and hearty grains are a `CropBlock` whose upper half is the harvest (RES8). | Unit: `CropAdapterChoiceTest`: a `CropBlock` in `harvest/pick_to_age_0` gets the pick adapter, a plain `CropBlock` the crop adapter, an `AttachedStemBlock` the gourd adapter, a block of `harvest/never` none, and a `BonemealableBlock` that is no `CropBlock` the growth adapter only. Fails if: an adapter other than the first in the order is chosen. | target handler (farming) | agreed |
| CD-CRP-04 | If a block of a farming role's tag has no crop adapter for that role, then the role registry MUST leave the block out of that role's targets. | MUST | R73, failure sweep | A pack can tag any block. A guess could break a plant for good. The load line names such blocks once (CD-CRP-05), so the pack maker sees them and the log stays short. | Unit: `CropAdapterChoiceTest`: the facts of nether wart in the Cultivator's tag get no growth adapter, and the handler's match rule refuses a block with no adapter. Fails if: such a block matches. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CRP-05 | When the tags load or reload, Dynamics MUST log one INFO line for each farming role with the number of its tagged blocks for each crop adapter and the ids of its tagged blocks that no adapter reads. | MUST | R73, R65, quality sweep | A pack maintainer sees in one line which crops each role works, as the load lines of R65 show the roles (CD-CMP-10). With the crop mods of COHO 2.5.0, the "no adapter" lists of the defaults are empty. | Unit: `LoadLinesTest`: the line for given counts reads `Farming: harvester 2 blocks [crop 1, pick_to_age_1 1], no adapter []`. Analysis: the test pack's `crops` case starts a server with the crop mods of COHO 2.5.0 and reads both lines. Fails if: a line is missing or logged twice in one load, or the COHO case lists a block with no adapter. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CRP-06 | The claim registry MUST key a work claim of a farming role by its crop and its role, so that one crop holds at most one Cultivator claim and at most one Harvester claim at once. | MUST | R72, ADR 0005, ADR 0015 | The approver's leash flow puts both roles on one crop: "leash to a single crop and idle when it's fully grown, so when it's harvested, it will bonemeal again to full". With one exclusive claim per crop, a Cultivator that waits at a fully grown crop would keep the Harvester off it. Two workers of one farming role on one crop would only race. | Unit: `ClaimRegistryTest`: on one crop, a Cultivator claim and a Harvester claim are both granted, a second Cultivator claim is refused, and an exclusive claim on a furnace is unaffected. GameTest: the scene of CD-CRP-11. Fails if: the other farming role is refused, or a second claim of one farming role is granted. | claim registry | agreed |
| CD-CRP-07 | A leash binding of a farming role MUST reserve its crop against the work claims of its own role only. | MUST | R72, ADR 0005, ADR 0015 | As CD-CRP-06: a leashed Cultivator must not keep a Harvester off its crop. | Unit: `ClaimRegistryTest`: a Cultivator's binding refuses another Cultivator's claim and grants a Harvester's claim. GameTest: the scene of CD-CRP-11. Fails if: a binding of one farming role blocks the other role, or lets a second worker of its own role claim. | claim registry | agreed |
| CD-CRP-08 | If a binding of the same farming role already reserves a crop, then the work engine MUST refuse a bind of that role on the crop with the message of CD-LSH-05. | MUST | R72, R3, misuse sweep | A crop takes one leashed worker of each farming role, and a bind of the other role succeeds (CD-CRP-07). | Unit: `BindRulesTest`: a Cultivator bind on a crop that a Cultivator binding reserves is refused with "Already assigned to {Pokémon}", and a Harvester bind on that crop succeeds. Fails if: either result differs. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CRP-09 | While the cell of a crop that a binding of a farming role names holds air or a fluid above a block that is not air, the leash resets MUST keep the binding. | MUST | R72, R58, ADR 0015 | The approver: "so when it's harvested, it will bonemeal again to full". A player harvests by breaking the crop and planting again, and a harvest with no replant item leaves the cell empty (CD-HRV-08). CD-LSH-08 clears a binding whose target breaks, which would end the leash at each harvest by hand, so this row excepts the empty cell of a crop. Another block in the cell, or no block below it, still clears the binding as CD-LSH-08 does. | Unit: `PlotRuleTest`: air above farmland keeps the binding, and stone in the cell or air below it clears it. GameTest: in the scene of CD-CRP-11, the test breaks the wheat before the Harvester binds, waits 100 ticks and plants wheat again. Fails if: the binding is gone at tick 100, or the Cultivator is not Working within 40 ticks of the planting. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CRP-10 | When the crop of an auto-assigned farming worker has no demand for the worker's role, the work engine MUST release the worker's work claim within 20 ticks. | MUST | R72, R44 | The approver: "it would go crop to crop, using bonemeal until the crop is fully grown and move on". CD-ASG-12 keeps a worker with no demand in Standby with its claim, and CD-ASG-19 moves it after 200 ticks, so a field would cost 10 s at each crop. This row excepts the farming roles, and the next scan pass gives the worker the next crop (CD-ASG-03). | Unit: `WorkerMachineTest`: an auto-assigned worker of a farming handler that has no demand while Working or on arrival releases its claim at once, and a leashed one enters Standby. GameTest: a level 50 Diglett and three wheat crops at age 0 in a row. Fails if: a crop is below age 7 after 2,400 ticks, the Diglett holds two claims at once, or it holds a claim on a fully grown crop for more than 20 ticks. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CRP-11 | While the crop of a leashed farming worker has no demand for the worker's role, the work engine MUST keep the worker in Standby at its work spot, with its claim and its binding. | MUST | R72, CD-ASG-12, CD-ASG-20 | The approver: "leash to a single crop and idle when it's fully grown, so when it's harvested, it will bonemeal again to full", and the Harvester has the "same leash behavior" (ruling 3). | GameTest: one wheat crop at age 0, a Diglett bound to it as a Cultivator, a Bulbasaur bound to it as a Harvester, and a Magnemite bound to a chest as a Magnet. Fails if: within 1,200 ticks of the Bulbasaur's bind the chest holds fewer than 2 wheat, a worker loses its binding, the Diglett is not in Standby while the wheat is fully grown, or it is not Working within 20 ticks after a replant. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CRP-12 | When the crop of a Quick Work order of a farming role has no demand for that role after its worker worked it, the work engine MUST end the order within 20 ticks, with the action-bar message "{Pokémon} stopped working: {target} needs no more work". | MUST | R72, R63 | Ruling 4 of R72 gives both roles Quick Work as auto-assign roles. An order is one-shot (R63), and a crop is done when it is grown or harvested, where CD-QWK-19 would keep the Cobblemon waiting for 200 ticks. | Unit: `QuickWorkRulesTest`: a farming order whose target lost its demand after Working ends with the reason `no_demand`. GameTest: an owner sends out a Bulbasaur at fully grown wheat. Fails if: the wheat is not harvested and replanted, or the order still runs 21 ticks after the harvest. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CRP-13 | If a work claim or a binding of the order's farming role already holds the crop, then the work engine MUST NOT start a Quick Work order on it, and it sends the owner the action-bar message "{target} is already being worked". | MUST NOT | R72, R63, CD-QWK-07 | CD-QWK-07 refuses an order on a target that an exclusive claim holds, and a farming claim is not exclusive (CD-CRP-06). A claim of the other farming role does not refuse the order. | Unit: `QuickWorkRulesTest`: a Harvester order on a crop with a Harvester claim is refused with `claimed`, and one on a crop with a Cultivator claim starts. Fails if: either result differs. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CRP-14 | The farming handlers MUST make no sound: they post no level event, play no sound, and call no block's use method and no `Level.destroyBlock`. | MUST | R72, CD-FX-09, RES8 | Ruling 4: "Work stays silent". Level event 1505 plays `BONE_MEAL_USE`, `destroyBlock` posts level event 2001, which plays the break sound, and the picks of the sweet berry bush, Farmer's Delight's tomato, cave vines and Cobblemon's berry block play sounds (RES8). | Unit: `SilenceTest` finds no `levelEvent`, `playSound`, `destroyBlock`, `useWithoutItem`, `useItemOn` or `attemptShear` in the sources under `handler/farming/`. Demo: check 61. Fails if: a source names one, or the Demo hears a sound from the work. | effects | agreed |
| CD-CRP-15 | If a worker that the work engine directs falls on farmland, then the mod MUST cancel the trample of that farmland. | MUST | R72, RES8, misuse sweep | Farming workers walk among crops. `FarmBlock.fallOn` lets a mob trample farmland while `mobGriefing` is on, which turns it to dirt and drops the crop, and NeoForge first posts the cancelable `BlockEvent.FarmlandTrampleEvent` (RES8). Wild Cobblemon and players keep the vanilla rule. | Unit: `TrampleGuardTest`: the guard cancels for a directed worker, and not for a wild Cobblemon or a player. Inspect: the listener asks `WorkEngine.isDirected`. Fails if: a directed worker tramples, or another entity's trample is canceled. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CRP-16 | The player guide MUST hold a section "Farming" that names the Cultivator and the Harvester, their leash behaviour, the drops on the ground beside the crop, the apricorn rule and the two target tags. | MUST | R72, R73, CD-REL-08 | Players learn where the harvest goes and how a Magnet collects it. | Inspect: `PlayerGuideTest` finds the section and each of these names. Fails if: the section or a name is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-CRP-17 | `docs/modpack-integration.md` MUST list the two target tags, the adapter tags and the crop families of the adapter table, with the versions of the COHO 2.5.0 crop mods that RES8 read. | MUST | R73, RES8 | Pack makers add crops through the tags, and the versions say what was checked. | Inspect: a unit test finds a section "Farming roles" that names `cultivator_targets`, `harvester_targets`, each adapter tag and each version of the table. Fails if: one is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-CRP-18 | Where a crop mod of the adapter table is loaded, the farming roles MUST grow and harvest each of its crop families as the adapter table states. | MUST | R73, RES8 | The approver: "COHO's modpack is a good example of many different modded crops, so we can start with compatibility for those." The approver tests actively on the COHO server (the GameTest ruling of 2026-11-11), so the modded families get a Demo, not a GameTest run. | Demo: check 62 on a COHO 2.5.0 server: for one crop of each family of the table, a Ground-type grows it to full, a Grass-type harvests it, and the drops, the replant and the state after the harvest match the table. Fails if: a family differs from the table, or no worker works it. | target handler (farming) | agreed |
| CD-CRP-19 | The main source set MUST NOT name a class of a crop mod other than Cobblemon. | MUST NOT | R73, RES8, ADR 0015 | The adapters read modded crops through vanilla classes, block state properties and tags, so no crop mod needs a compat package, and no crop mod can crash the game by its absence (CD-CMP-06). | Unit: `CompatImportsTest`: no main source names `vectorwing`, `com.pam`, `net.mehvahdjukaar`, `blusunrize`, `com.aetherteam`, `io.github.razordevs`, `net.potionstudios`, `quek.undergarden`, `com.hollingsworth`, `com.klikli_dev`, `net.regions_unexplored`, `ianm1647` or `com.jorgaomc`. Fails if: one appears. | build | agreed |
| CD-CRP-20 | Each tick of a Working farming worker MUST run at most one growth use or one harvest. | MUST | R72, CD-PERF-01, quality sweep | A use or a harvest touches one crop, so the cost of farming grows with the number of workers, not with the number of crops, and the block scan stays within CD-ASG-07. | Unit: `GrowthPaceTest` and `HarvestClockTest` give at most one action per tick for every level and every `harvest_ticks` of 1 or more. Inspect: the farming handlers use `ScanKind.BLOCK` and add no scan of their own. Fails if: a tick runs two actions, or a farming handler scans. | target handler (farming) | agreed |
| CD-CRP-21 | When a Cobblemon fits both farming roles, Quick Work MUST pick, of the two, the role that has demand on the aimed crop. | MUST | R72, R63, CD-QWK-03 | A Grass/Ground Cobblemon, such as Torterra, fits both roles. | CD-CRP-22 | When a Cobblemon fits both farming roles on a clicked crop, the Work Leash MUST bind the role of the Cobblemon's primary type. | MUST | R72, CD-LSH-16 | The role must not depend on the crop's age at the click. Fails if: either bind differs. | work engine | agreed |

### OPT: optional mods and zero setup (1.2)

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-OPT-01 | Where a mod of the table of ADR-0016 is loaded, Dynamics MUST work each target of ADR-0020 for that mod with the built-in role files, tags and data maps, and with no config key, data pack or command of the server admin. | MUST | R78, R79, G14, ADR 0016 | The approver: "there should be little to no setup that the server admin needs to do to get everything working. we should have values for everything". Tags and data maps exist for overrides only. | Analysis: the test-pack case `mods` (CD-OPT-15) starts a server with the default config and every mod that the test pack can fetch or copy, and each compat line (CD-OPT-11) names every adapter as found. Inspect: the section "Install" of `docs/modpack-integration.md` names no setup step for a supported mod. Fails if: an adapter is missing with the defaults, or the page asks the admin to set a key or add a data pack. | build | agreed |
| CD-OPT-02 | The role registry MUST skip a role file whose `neoforge:conditions` fail, before it parses the role, and count it neither as loaded nor as disabled. | MUST | R78, R79, RES9, ADR 0016 | NeoForge 21.1.265 applies no conditions to `SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener`, so `RoleRegistry.apply` tests them with `ICondition.conditionsMatched(makeConditionalOps(), json)` (RES9). A role of an absent mod then makes no disabled line that reads as a setup problem. | Unit: `RoleRegistryConditionsTest`: a file whose `neoforge:mod_loaded` names an absent mod is skipped and in neither count, a file that names a present mod loads, and a file with no conditions loads. Fails if: a skipped file is parsed, loaded or counted as disabled, or a file with passing conditions is skipped. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-OPT-03 | Each built-in role file whose handler a compat package registers MUST hold the condition `neoforge:mod_loaded` of that package's mod. | MUST | R78, ADR 0016 | The file of a mod's role loads only with that mod (decision 2 of ADR-0016). | Unit: `BuiltInRoleFilesTest`: each role file whose handler belongs to a compat package has a `neoforge:conditions` entry of type `neoforge:mod_loaded` with that package's mod id. Fails if: such a file lacks the condition or names another mod. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-OPT-04 | When the role files load, Dynamics MUST log one INFO line that names each role file that its conditions skipped, with the mod ids that it needs. | MUST | R65, R78, ADR 0016, quality sweep | A pack maintainer still sees which roles a mod would add, as the load lines of R65 show the roles (CD-CMP-10). | Unit: `LoadLinesTest`: the line for two skipped files reads `Roles not loaded, their mods are absent: 2 [cobblemon_dynamics:heater_mekanism (mekanism), cobblemon_dynamics:stoker_railcraft (railcraft)]`. Analysis: the log of the test pack's `smoke` server holds the line once. Fails if: the line is missing, logged twice in one load, or names a file that loaded. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-OPT-05 | Outside the compat package `compat/<modid>/` and the mixin package `mixin/compat/<modid>/` of a mod of the table of ADR-0016, the main source set MUST NOT name a class of that mod. | MUST NOT | R78, ADR 0016, failure sweep | A class that names an absent mod crashes the game when it loads (CD-CMP-06). | Unit: `CompatImportsTest`: for each mod of the table, no main source outside its two packages names its package prefixes. Fails if: one does. | build | agreed |
| CD-OPT-06 | Where a mod of the table of ADR-0016 is absent, Dynamics MUST load no class of its compat package. | MUST | R78, ADR 0016, failure sweep | As CD-CMP-06, CD-CMP-07 and CD-LND-13 for the earlier compat packages. | Analysis: the test pack's `smoke` server, which holds none of these mods, starts with no error that names a compat class. Inspect: each package registers only behind `ModList.isLoaded`. Fails if: the server logs such an error, or a registration has no guard. | compat | agreed |
| CD-OPT-07 | `neoforge.mods.toml` MUST declare each mod of the table of ADR-0016 that the code names as an optional dependency with the version range `*`, except Create and AE2, whose ranges CD-PLAT-05 pins. | MUST | R78, R80, R83, ADR 0016 | As for the claim mods (CD-QWK-34, CD-LND-14): NeoForge checks no version, and a hook that fails turns its target off. CD-PLAT-05 keeps the ranges of Create and AE2 (R83). | Unit: `ModsTomlTest` reads the optional entries and their ranges. Fails if: a mod is missing, required, or has another range. | build | agreed |
| CD-OPT-08 | The mixin plugin MUST skip the mixins of the package `mixin/compat/<modid>/` while that mod is absent. | MUST | R78, ADR 0016 | Mixin fails its required config on a missing target class. | Unit: `MixinPluginTest`: the package map holds each mixin package of 1.2 with its mod id, and a mixin of an absent mod is skipped with its reason. Fails if: a package is missing from the map, or its mixin applies without its mod. | compat | agreed |
| CD-OPT-09 | If a mixin that a role or a target of an optional mod needs did not apply, then the role registry MUST turn off that role or that target and log one warning. | MUST | R78, ADR 0016, failure sweep | A later version of a mod must not crash the game, as for Create (CD-WHL-10). A target inside a shared handler, such as Easy Magic's table for the Muse, turns off alone. | Unit: `MixinGateTest`: with the applied flag of each mixin of 1.2 false, its role or its target is off, and one warning names it. Fails if: the role or target stays on, another role turns off, or the warning repeats. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-OPT-10 | If a handler or a hook of an optional mod throws an `Exception` or a `LinkageError`, then Dynamics MUST turn off that handler's roles until the role files load again, log one warning per handler, and keep the game running. | MUST | R78, ADR 0016, failure sweep | A renamed member of a mod's internal class must not crash the game (decision 6 of ADR-0016). | Unit: a handler that throws `NoSuchMethodError` in `hasDemand` turns its roles off with one warning, and the other roles keep working. Fails if: the game crashes, another role stops, or the warning repeats. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-OPT-11 | When tags and data maps load, Dynamics MUST log one INFO line for each loaded mod of the table of ADR-0016 that has a compat package, which names each of its adapters as found or missing. | MUST | R80, R92, ADR 0016, quality sweep | Ruling 6 of R80: the load lines report each adapter as found or not found, so a pack maintainer sees at once why a target does not work. Analysis: the test-pack case `mods` reads the lines. Fails if: a line is missing, logged twice in one load, or names an adapter as found whose block id, mixin or class is missing. | compat | agreed |
| CD-OPT-12 | Each entry of a block, a fluid or an item of an optional mod in a built-in tag or data map MUST be optional: `"required": false` in a tag, and its own condition on that mod in a data map. | MUST | R78, R83, ADR 0016 | A tag or data map must load without the mod (decision 4 of ADR-0016), as the crop tags do (CD-CRP-01). Fails if: an entry is required or has no condition. | build | agreed |
| CD-OPT-13 | The jar MUST NOT contain a class of a mod of the table of ADR-0016. | MUST NOT | R78, ADR 0016 | The mods are compile-only dependencies, and several are All Rights Reserved. | Inspect: `verifyJar` refuses each package prefix of the table. Fails if: the jar holds such a class. | build | agreed |
| CD-OPT-14 | The build MUST compile against each mod of the table of ADR-0016 that the code names as a compile-only dependency with `transitive = false`, at the version that the 1.2 plan pins. | MUST | R80, ADR 0016 | Ruling 5 of R80 pins each mod's version, so the tested version is the compiled one. | Inspect: `build.gradle` lists each dependency with its pinned version, and `docs/modpack-integration.md` names the same versions. Fails if: a version differs, or a dependency is transitive. | build | agreed |
| CD-OPT-15 | The test pack MUST hold a case `mods` that starts one dedicated server with the built jar, the pinned set and every mod of the table of ADR-0016 that it can fetch from Modrinth or copy from `TESTPACK_LOCAL_JARS`, with their libraries. | MUST | R80, ADR 0016 | Ruling 6 of R80: one test-pack boot with every supported mod installed. | CD-OPT-16 | The player guide MUST hold the sections "Lumberjack", "Experience Collector", "Soul Collector", "Freezer" and "Heater", and a section "Other mods" that names each supported mod with the roles that work its machines. | MUST | R76, R77, CD-REL-08 | Players learn where the logs, the XP and the ice go, and which machines of their pack the roles reach. | Inspect: `PlayerGuideTest` finds each section and each mod name of the table of ADR-0016. ### FIX: fixes of today's behaviour (1.2)

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-FIX-01 | The Stoker handler MUST NOT claim a block of `#cobblemon_dynamics:stoker/never`, whose default holds `aether:freezer` as an optional entry. | MUST NOT | R80, RES11, ADR 0020 | Ruling 2 of R80: "The Stoker must exclude the Aether Freezer." The Aether's Freezer extends the vanilla furnace and burns Icestone, so a Fire-type would fuel an ice machine (RES11). A pack can add other machines. | Unit: `FurnaceHandlerTest`: a furnace block entity whose state is in the tag does not match, and one outside it does. `StokerTagFilesTest` finds `aether:freezer` with `required` false. Fails if: a tagged block matches, or the entry is missing or required. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-FIX-02 | The Crystal Tender handler MUST count a bud of `#c:clusters` on a face as a bud that can grow only while its integer `stage` or `age` property is below its highest value. | MUST | R80, RES11, RES13, ADR 0020 | Ruling 2 of R80: "The Crystal Tender's type-gem core demand never clears, because full clusters are in `#c:buds`." Cobblemon puts every stage of its type gem clusters in both `#c:buds` and `#c:clusters` (RES13). A vanilla amethyst cluster is in `#c:clusters` only, so amethyst keeps its rule. | Unit: `BuddingRulesTest`: a type gem cluster at stage 0 on a face of a type gem core gives demand, the same cluster at its last stage gives none, and a large amethyst bud gives demand. Fails if: a full cluster gives demand, or a growing bud gives none. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-FIX-03 | A harvest of a block of the data map `cobblemon_dynamics:harvest_loot` MUST NOT drop the block's own item. | MUST NOT | R80, RES11, RES13, ADR 0020 | Ruling 2 of R80: "A Twilight Forest berry or oreberry harvest must never drop the bush itself." The bush's block loot table drops the bush, so a harvest rolls the berry table of the data map instead (CD-FRM-03). | Unit: `HarvestPlanTest`: the plan of a fully grown blackberry bush holds the loot of `twilightforest:blocks/blackberry_bush_berries` only, and no `twilightforest:blackberry_bush` item. Fails if: the plan holds the bush item, or the loot of the block's own table. | target handler (farming) | agreed |
| CD-FIX-04 | The Cultivator handler MUST NOT claim a block of `#cobblemon_dynamics:cultivator/never`, whose default holds `#mysticalagriculture:crops` as an optional entry. | MUST NOT | R80, RES12, ADR 0020 | Ruling 2 of R80: Mystical Agriculture's `isBonemealSuccess` always returns false, so a Cultivator spent Work Stamina there with no growth. "Respect the mod's design: no growth there." A tag cannot remove an entry of `#minecraft:crops`, so the handler checks a tag of its own. | Unit: `CropAdapterChoiceTest`: a block of the never tag gets no growth adapter. `FarmingTagFilesTest` finds `#mysticalagriculture:crops` with `required` false. Fails if: a tagged block matches, or the entry is missing or required. | target handler (farming) | agreed |
| CD-FIX-05 | Where the mod `easymagic` is loaded, the Muse role MUST work `easymagic:enchanting_table` and add its bookshelves to the power that `ModEnchantmentMenu` counts, within the menu's maximum of 15, through a mixin on its power method. | MUST | R80, RES12, ADR 0020 | Ruling 2 of R80. Easy Magic replaces the vanilla table with its own block, and its menu overrides `slotsChanged` with no `super` call, so the Muse found no target (RES12). | Unit: `MuseBonusTest`: a power of 10 with a level 100 Muse reads 15. GameTest: in the compat run, a Muse at Easy Magic's table with 5 bookshelves gives the offers of 15 bookshelves while a player has the menu open. Fails if: the power differs, or the offers stay at 5 bookshelves. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-FIX-06 | Where Enchanting Infuser is loaded, the Muse role MUST add nothing to an infuser while Apothic Enchanting is also loaded, and otherwise work both infusers and add its bookshelves to the power that `InfuserMenu.getAvailablePower` returns, within the infuser's maximum of 15, through a mixin at that method's return. | MUST | R80, R81, RES12, ADR 0020 | Ruling 2 of R80. With Apothic Enchanting, the infuser counts this power as Eterna, and the Muse gives only Quanta there (CD-MUS-07). The approver: "the fairy types should give Quanta, not Eterna, if apotheosis is active" (decision 30, R81). The infuser's menu is no `EnchantmentMenu`, and its power method is private (RES12). | Unit: `MuseBonusTest` for the infuser's maximum without Apothic Enchanting, and for no bonus with it. GameTest: in the compat run, a level 100 Muse raises the available power of an infuser with no bookshelves to 15. Fails if: the power differs, passes the maximum, or rises with Apothic Enchanting. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-FIX-07 | The Superheater handler MUST report demand while a block of `#cobblemon_dynamics:superheater/heat_users` sits directly above the burner, as well as on Create's own rule of CD-BRN-03. | MUST | R80, RES11, RES12, ADR 0020 | Ruling 2 of R80: "Broaden the Superheater demand rule (CD-BRN-03) to any heat user above the burner." The default tag holds `create:basin`, `create:fluid_tank`, `farmersdelight:cooking_pot` and `farmersdelight:skillet` (through Create: Central Kitchen), the distillation tank of Create: Diesel Generators and the heat pipe of Create: New Age, each entry of another mod optional. | Unit: `HeatGateTest`: a block of the tag above the burner gives demand, a basin gives demand, and a stone block gives none. GameTest: a Dratini at a filled burner under a test block that the test data pack adds to the tag. Fails if: a heat user gives no demand, or another block gives demand. | target handler (Create) | agreed |

### LUM: Lumberjack (1.2)

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-LUM-01 | The role registry MUST enable the Lumberjack role, with the handler `cobblemon_dynamics:lumberjack`, for the Bug type, with its auto-assign flag off. | MUST | R76, R79, G13, ADR 0017 | The approver: "Lumberjack: make it leash only, then approved". A leash-only role scans nothing and takes no Quick Work (CD-QWK-03). | Unit: `BuiltInRoleFilesTest` reads `lumberjack.json`: handler `cobblemon_dynamics:lumberjack`, types `bug`, `auto_assign` false, no `quick_work`, priority 0. Fails if: a value differs. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-LUM-02 | The Lumberjack handler MUST match a log of `#cobblemon_dynamics:lumberjack_targets`, whose default holds `#minecraft:logs`, and a block of `#minecraft:saplings`. | MUST | R76, R79, RES9, ADR 0017 | A player binds a worker to a grown tree, or to the sapling of a new wood line. A pack narrows or widens the logs with the tag. | Unit: `TreeRulesTest`: an oak log and an oak sapling match, and stone does not. Fails if: either result differs. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-03 | The Lumberjack handler MUST resolve a clicked log to the trunk base cell, the lowest log that the tree search of CD-LUM-04 finds from it with the least coordinates on a tie, and a clicked sapling to itself or to the corner of its 2 × 2 of saplings. | MUST | R79, RES9, ADR 0017 | The coordinator: "The binding goes to a tree and resolves to its trunk base cell." An acacia trunk steps sideways, so the base comes from the search, not from a column. | Unit: `TreeRulesTest`: a click on a branch of an acacia resolves to its lowest log, and a click on any log of a 2 × 2 dark oak to its corner with the least coordinates. Fails if: another cell results. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-04 | The tree search MUST take as the tree's logs the blocks of the base log's own block id that it reaches from the base through the 26 neighbours, at most `max_logs` (default 256), and find no tree when a log is next to a cell of a chunk that is not loaded. Such a cell keeps the leaves near it as a log does. | MUST | R79, R82, RES9, ADR 0017, limits sweep | Acacia logs step diagonally. A stripped log, a wood block or a log of another kind never joins, so a log build beside a tree stays. The coordinator: "Skip ... trees over 256 logs." A cell that is not loaded could hold more of the tree, or a log that keeps a leaf (R82). | Unit: `TreeSearchTest`: a fancy oak gives all its logs, a stripped log beside it stays out, and a tree of 257 logs gives no tree. A log next to a cell that is not loaded, also through a corner, gives no tree. Fails if: a log of the tree is missed, another block joins, a tree above the cap is found, or a tree next to a cell that is not loaded is found. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-05 | The tree search MUST take as the tree's leaves each `LeavesBlock` with `PERSISTENT` false that lies within 6 face steps through leaves of a log of the tree, and not within 6 such steps of a log outside the tree, at most `max_leaves` (default 1,024). | MUST | R76, R79, RES9, ADR 0017, limits sweep | The approver: "cut the whole tree with the leaves". These are the leaves that vanilla would let decay once the tree is gone, so a neighbouring tree keeps its own. Only leaves carry a natural-or-placed signal (RES9). | Unit: `CanopyRuleTest`: two oaks whose canopies touch each give only their own leaves, and a tree with 1,025 such leaves gives no tree. Fails if: a leaf of the other tree is taken, a leaf of the tree is missed, or a tree above the cap is found. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-06 | The Lumberjack handler MUST NOT break a leaf whose `PERSISTENT` property is true. | MUST NOT | R79, R82, RES9, ADR 0017, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "Never: player-placed leaves". A player can place a leaf in a cell of a fell between two of its ticks (R82). `getStateForPlacement` sets `PERSISTENT` true on every leaf that a player places (RES9). | Unit: `CanopyRuleTest`: persistent leaves inside a canopy stay out of the plan. `FellPlanTest`: a leaf that is persistent when its step comes stays. GameTest: a felled oak with 4 placed oak leaves on it. Fails if: a placed leaf breaks. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-07 | If a tree has fewer than 3 leaves of CD-LUM-05, then the tree search MUST find no tree. | MUST | R79, RES9, ADR 0017, misuse sweep | Logs carry no natural-or-placed signal, so a trunk with no natural canopy is a build. MineColonies' forester uses the same test (RES9). | Unit: `TreeSearchTest`: a column of oak logs with 2 natural leaves gives no tree, and with 3 gives a tree. Fails if: either result differs. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-08 | The Lumberjack handler MUST NOT fell a tree when a block of the tree or a block next to one is in `#cobblemon_dynamics:lumberjack/never`. | MUST NOT | R79, RES9, RES11, ADR 0017, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "Never: ... Pam's fruit trees (the `harvest/pick_to_age_0` blocks), apricorn trees, Twilight Forest magic trees", and "Skip huge nether fungi". The default holds `#cobblemon_dynamics:harvest/pick_to_age_0`, `#cobblemon:apricorns`, `#cobblemon:apricorn_logs`, `cobblemon:apricorn_leaves`, the four magic log cores of the Twilight Forest, `minecraft:bee_nest`, `minecraft:beehive`, `#minecraft:crimson_stems`, `#minecraft:warped_stems`, `#minecraft:mangrove_logs`, `unusualend:chorus_cane_block` and `betterarcheology:rotten_log`. | Unit: `TreeSearchTest`: an oak with a Pam's apple below its leaves, an apricorn tree, an oak with a bee nest, a crimson fungus and a mangrove give no tree. `LumberjackTagFilesTest` finds each default, and each entry of another mod optional. Fails if: one of them gives a tree, or an entry is missing or required. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-09 | The fell plan MUST take each block next to the tree, or next to another block of the plan, that could not survive with the tree's cells empty, by its `canSurvive` against such a view of the level. | MUST | R79, RES9, ADR 0017 | The coordinator: "Attached blocks (cocoa, vines, propagules, Pam fruit) need a rule." Without it, vanilla's shape update breaks cocoa, vines, hanging propagules, snow layers and torches through `Level.destroyBlock`, with its sound, and drops their loot where they hung (RES9). | Unit: `ShellRuleTest`: cocoa on a jungle log, a chain of 3 vines below a leaf and a torch on a log join the plan, and a flower on the grass beside the trunk does not. Fails if: an attached block is missed, or a block that can stand alone joins. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-10 | A leashed Lumberjack MUST have demand only while a tree of CD-LUM-04 to CD-LUM-08 stands on its base cell. | MUST | R79, ADR 0017 | The coordinator: "The binding then waits on the sapling until it is fully grown again, like a bound Harvester." At a sapling or an empty cell, the worker waits in Standby with its claim and its binding (CD-ASG-12). | Unit: `TreeRulesTest`: a grown oak on the base gives demand, and a sapling, air above grass and a tree of the never tag give none. Fails if: either result differs. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-11 | While a Lumberjack is Working, the handler MUST fell its tree once, T ticks for each log of the tree after its work started. The claim stays through the regrowth, so the fell comes once for each start of work (R82). A level 67 worker matches a player with an iron axe, at 10 ticks per log (BM6). A big tree takes longer, and Work Stamina drains only through Working (CD-STA-06). | Unit: `FellClockTest`: for a level 100 worker, an oak of 5 logs falls on tick 25 after the start and not on tick 24. | CD-LUM-12 | In the tick that a fell starts, the Lumberjack handler MUST ask the use question and the break question of every block of the fell, and the use question of the base cell, and fell nothing when one refuses. | MUST | R79, R73, ADR 0014, ADR 0017, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "Ask the claim mods' 'may break' check for each block, as the Harvester does." A tree can cross a chunk border into another team's claim. The handler asks each claim mod once for each chunk and block id of the fell, which gives the same answers. | Unit: `FellPlanTest`: the plan lists every log, leaf and shell block with its questions, and the cache asks once per chunk and block id. | CD-LUM-13 | If a question of CD-LUM-12 refuses, then the Lumberjack handler MUST report no demand on that tree to that worker for 6,000 ticks. | MUST | R79, ADR 0017, failure sweep | As a refused harvest waits (ADR-0015). Fails if: the worker asks again sooner. | target handler (trees) | agreed |
| CD-LUM-14 | The fell MUST remove its blocks with `Level.setBlock` to each cell's fluid, the shell first with `UPDATE_CLIENTS` and `UPDATE_KNOWN_SHAPE`, then the leaves and the logs from the top with `UPDATE_ALL`, at most `blocks_per_tick` (default 128) blocks per tick. | MUST | R76, RES9, ADR 0017, quality sweep | The approver: "cut the whole tree with the leaves". Inspect: `LumberjackHandler` reads the loot of each block and pops each stack in its own `try`, and ends the run in a `finally`. GameTest: in the scene of CD-LUM-17, an item frame with a diamond on the trunk and a rail that rises onto the base cell. ### XPC: Experience Collector (1.2)

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-XPC-01 | Dynamics MUST register the Experience Collector block `cobblemon_dynamics:experience_collector`, with a block entity and an item, and list the item in its creative tab after the Charge Cell. | MUST | R76, R79, G13, ADR 0018 | The approver: "build an Experience Collector container for the mod, so it doesn't have to rely on other mods and will fit better with vanilla". | GameTest: place the block, read its block entity, and build the creative tab of CD-UI-16. Fails if: the block, its block entity or its item is missing, or the tab lists the item elsewhere. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-02 | When a player places an Experience Collector, the block MUST turn its front toward the player, as a furnace does. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: "the front faces the placer". The back and bottom outputs then face away from the player. | GameTest: a player who faces north places a collector. Fails if: its `FACING` is not south. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-03 | The Experience Collector MUST store from 0 to `experienceCollector.capacity` XP, and take no XP while it holds that capacity or more. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018, limits sweep | The coordinator: "Capacity: propose one, for example 100,000 XP, as a config key." A collector that holds more than a lowered capacity keeps its XP. | Unit: `CollectorStoreTest`: a store at 99,990 of 100,000 takes 10 and refuses 1 more, and a store of 120,000 after the capacity falls to 100,000 keeps 120,000 and takes none. Fails if: an insert passes the capacity, or XP is lost. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-04 | When a player right-clicks an Experience Collector, the block MUST open a screen that shows the stored XP in points and as the level that a player at level 0 would reach with it, and a button "Collect experience". | MUST | R76, R79, ADR 0018 | The approver: "it should have an interface on right-click that has a collect experience button". | Unit: `XpLevelsTest`: 0, 7, 352 and 100,000 XP give the levels 0, 1, 16 and 166. Demo: check 64. Fails if: a level differs, or the screen lacks the points, the level or the button. | client | agreed |
| CD-XPC-05 | When a player presses "Collect experience", the server MUST give all stored XP to that player through `Player.giveExperiencePoints` and set the store to 0 in the same tick, only while vanilla's checks of the open menu pass, and put the XP back into the store if the grant leaves the player's total XP as it was. | MUST | R76, R79, R86, RES13, ADR 0018, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "A server-checked payload gives all stored XP to that player." The button sends vanilla's container button packet, whose handler checks the open menu, the spectator mode and `stillValid` before `clickMenuButton` (RES13). NeoForge's `giveExperiencePoints` posts `PlayerXpEvent.XpChange`, which a listener can cancel after the store gave its XP, so the XP goes back (R86). | Unit: `CollectorMenuTest`: `clickMenuButton` with 1,234 stored gives 1,234 and leaves 0, a second press gives nothing, and a grant that leaves the player's total as it was keeps the 1,234 in the store. GameTest: a player 9 blocks away with the menu open presses the button. Fails if: the player gets no XP or another amount, the store keeps XP after a grant, a canceled grant empties the store, or the far player gets any. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-06 | The menu of an Experience Collector MUST sync the stored XP to the screen in two data slots of 16 bits each. | MUST | RES13, ADR 0018, limits sweep | `ClientboundContainerSetDataPacket` writes each value as a short, so one slot shows at most 32,767 (RES13). | Unit: `CollectorMenuTest`: 100,000, 65,535 and 65,536 XP split into two slots that join back to the same values. Fails if: a value joins back to another number. | client | agreed |
| CD-XPC-07 | The Experience Collector MUST expose a fluid handler on its back face and its bottom face only, which holds the fluid of CD-XPC-08 with the stored XP × its ratio in mB. | MUST | R76, R79, ADR 0018 | The approver: "an output on the back and bottom of the collector for pipes". | GameTest: a collector that faces south with 100 XP and a test fluid of `c:experience`. Fails if: the north face or the bottom face has no handler with 2,000 mB, or another face or a null side has one. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-08 | After each tag load, the Experience Collector MUST resolve its fluid as `create_enchantment_industry:experience` at 1 mB per XP when the fluid registry holds it, otherwise as the first fluid of `#c:experience` at 20 mB per XP, otherwise as none, and log one INFO line that names the choice. | MUST | R79, RES9, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: "Create Enchantment Industry's `create_enchantment_industry:experience` (1 mB per XP) first, otherwise the first fluid in `c:experience` (20 mB per XP)". The fluid of CEI is not in `c:experience` (RES9). With none, the faces expose no handler, and the button still works. | Unit: `XpFluidChoiceTest`: CEI's fluid gives itself at 1, `sophisticatedcore:xp_still` alone in the tag gives itself at 20, and neither gives none. Analysis: the test-pack case `mods` logs the choice of CEI's fluid. Fails if: another fluid or ratio results, or the line is missing. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-09 | A drain of A mB from an Experience Collector MUST remove floor(A ÷ ratio) XP and give that XP × ratio mB. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018, failure sweep | No part of an XP point is lost or made by a pipe that asks for an odd amount. | Unit: `XpFluidTankTest`: at 20 mB per XP, a drain of 30 mB gives 20 mB and removes 1 XP, and a drain of 19 mB gives nothing. Fails if: a drain gives another amount, or removes another number of points. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-10 | The fluid handler of an Experience Collector MUST NOT accept a fluid. | MUST NOT | R79, ADR 0018, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "Pipes cannot insert into it." | Unit: `XpFluidTankTest`: `fill` of the XP fluid and of water returns 0, and `isFluidValid` is false. Fails if: a fill returns more than 0. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-11 | Where experiencelib is loaded, the back face and the bottom face of an Experience Collector MUST also expose `ExperienceLibCapabilities.EXPERIENCE` with a handler that only drains: `extractExperience` takes up to the stored XP and honours its simulate flag, `receiveExperience` returns 0, and `setExperience` and `setCapacity` change nothing. | MUST | R79, RES9, RES13, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: "Check whether Applied Experienced can import XP directly through experiencelib's XP capability, and add that when it can." Its import bus reads `getExperience()` and calls `extractExperience` on the block that it faces (RES13). | Unit: `ExperienceLibHandlerTest` over a store of 500: a simulated extract of 200 removes nothing, a real one removes 200, a receive of 100 returns 0, and `setExperience(0, 0)` keeps 300. Demo: check 64 with Applied Experienced 1.3.2. Fails if: a result differs, or the import bus imports nothing. | compat | agreed |
| CD-XPC-12 | The Experience Collector MUST give a comparator signal of 0 when it is empty and 1 + floor(14 × stored ÷ capacity) otherwise. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: "It gives comparator output." The form copies the Charge Cell (CD-GEN-10). | Unit: 0, 1, 50,000, 99,999 and 100,000 XP of 100,000 give 0, 1, 8, 14 and 15. Fails if: a signal differs. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-13 | When an Experience Collector is broken, also by an explosion, and also by a player in creative mode while it holds XP, it MUST drop itself as an item that keeps the stored XP in the data component `cobblemon_dynamics:stored_experience`, shows it in its tooltip and gives it to the collector that it places. | MUST | R79, R86, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: "It keeps its XP on the item when broken, like the Charge Cell." An explosion with drop decay, as a creeper's, rolls the loot condition `minecraft:survives_explosion` at 1 in its radius, and a break in creative mode drops no loot, so both lost the XP (R86). A collector that holds no XP drops nothing in creative mode, as a shulker box with no items. | Unit: `CollectorFilesTest`: the loot table has no condition. GameTest: break a collector with 12,345 XP and place its drop. GameTest: an explosion of radius 3 with drop decay breaks a full collector, four times, and a player in creative mode breaks a collector with 12,345 XP and an empty one. Fails if: the component is not 12,345, the tooltip does not read "12,345 / 100,000 XP", the placed collector holds another amount, an exploded collector drops no item with all its XP, the creative break drops no item with 12,345 XP, or the empty collector drops an item. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-14 | Dynamics MUST register the Experience Collector recipe of the Recipes table. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: "Propose a vanilla-ingredient recipe." | GameTest: craft the collector from its exact inputs, then try the same grid without the hopper. Fails if: the first grid makes no collector, or the second makes one. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-15 | The Experience Collector MUST open its screen only through its block's use path, so that a canceled right-click opens nothing. | MUST | R79, CD-SEC-11, ADR 0018, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "Anyone who can open it can collect, like a chest. Claim mods protect it as a block." A claim mod cancels the right-click, as for a chest. | GameTest: an event listener cancels right-clicks on a collector, and a player clicks it. Fails if: the player gets a menu. | content | agreed |
| CD-XPC-16 | The Experience Collector MUST be in the block tag `minecraft:mineable/pickaxe`. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018 | A pickaxe breaks it at the speed of a machine, as for the Charge Cell. | Unit: the tag file holds the block. Fails if: it does not. | content | agreed |

### SOU: Soul Collector (1.2)

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-SOU-01 | The role registry MUST enable the Soul Collector role, with the handler `cobblemon_dynamics:soul_collector`, for the Ghost type, with its auto-assign flag off. | MUST | R76, R79, G13, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: "The Soul Collector is Work Leash only". | Unit: `BuiltInRoleFilesTest` reads `soul_collector.json`: handler `cobblemon_dynamics:soul_collector`, types `ghost`, `auto_assign` false, no `quick_work`, priority 0. Fails if: a value differs. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-SOU-02 | The Soul Collector handler MUST match an Experience Collector and no other block. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: it "binds ONLY to an Experience Collector", with no bottle mode and no direct target in the tanks of other mods. | Unit: the handler matches a collector, and refuses a chest, a Create fluid tank and a test tank of `c:experience`. Fails if: another block matches. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-03 | The claim registry MUST give the work claim of a Soul Collector as a shared claim, as for a logistics role (CD-CLM-10). | MUST | R66, R79, ADR 0018 | The approver's ruling on logistics: a container takes 3 to 5 workers. A collector holds no items, so only Soul Collectors share it. | Unit: `ClaimRegistryTest`: five Soul Collector claims on one collector are granted, and a sixth is refused with the default cap. Fails if: one of the five is refused, or the sixth is granted. | claim registry | agreed |
| CD-SOU-04 | The Soul Collector handler MUST take only the XP orbs whose position lies inside its worker's pasture work area. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018, limits sweep | The coordinator: "It takes XP orbs in the pasture's work area". The query copies the Magnet's (CD-MAG-04). | GameTest: orbs at 7 and at 9 blocks from the pasture block, along an axis. Fails if: the orb at 9 is taken, or the orb at 7 is not. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-05 | The Soul Collector handler MUST NOT take an orb while a player who is not a spectator stands within `player_distance` (default 8) blocks of it. | MUST NOT | R79, RES9, ADR 0018, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "leaves alone any orb within 8 blocks of a player, so it never takes a player's XP or Mending repairs." Vanilla makes an orb follow the nearest such player within 8 blocks (RES9). | Unit: `OrbRulesTest`: an orb 7.9 blocks from a player is skipped, an orb 8.1 blocks away is taken, and a spectator 2 blocks away stops nothing. Fails if: a result differs. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-06 | In each batch of 20 ticks, the Soul Collector handler MUST take at most ceil(U × L ÷ 100) orb units, nearest first, where U is the role file's `orb_units_per_second_at_level_100` (default 10). | MUST | R79, BM7, ADR 0018, limits sweep | The coordinator: "Its pace grows with level". A level 100 worker takes one unit per 2 ticks, as a player does (BM7). | Unit: `OrbRulesTest`: of 30 units in one batch, level 1 takes 1, level 50 takes 5 and level 100 takes 10, the nearest first. Fails if: a batch takes more, or skips a nearer unit for a farther one. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-07 | The Soul Collector handler MUST NOT lose or make XP: it adds to the collector exactly the XP that it takes from each orb, as `value` × `count` units. | MUST NOT | R79, R86, RES9, ADR 0018, failure sweep | An orb holds `value` × `count` XP, and an access transformer makes `count` public. A take from an orb with a count of 1 lowers its `value`, and an orb whose unit does not fit and whose count is above 1 stays (decision 17 of ADR-0018). A batch reads the capacity once, so a config reload in the batch changes nothing, and it puts each take into the collector before it changes the orb, only by the XP that the collector took (R86). | Unit: `OrbRulesTest`: an orb of value 5 and count 4 with room for 12 gives 10 XP and keeps count 2, an orb of value 50 and count 1 with room for 20 gives 20 and keeps value 30, and the first take cut to the 7 XP that a collector took gives 5 and keeps count 3. `CollectorStoreTest`: an insert under a capacity read once ignores a later change of the capacity. Fails if: the XP taken and the XP added differ, or an orb loses XP that the collector does not get. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-08 | The Soul Collector handler MUST report demand only while a takeable orb lies in the work area and its collector has room. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018 | A worker with nothing to take waits in Standby, and Work Stamina drains only while Working (CD-STA-06). | GameTest: a bound Gastly at an empty collector with no orb, then 5 orbs. Fails if: the Gastly Works with no orb, or is not Working within 40 ticks of the orbs. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-09 | The Soul Collector handler MUST query the orbs of its work area at most once every 20 ticks for each worker. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018, quality sweep | One query of the area per batch, as for the Magnet (CD-MAG-08). | Unit: `QueryClock` gives one query in each 20 ticks. Fails if: a worker queries twice in 20 ticks. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-10 | The Soul Collector handler MUST read `orb_units_per_second_at_level_100` (10) and `player_distance` (8) from its role file. | MUST | R79, ADR 0006 | Role numbers live in role files (CD-CFG-07). | Unit: `BuiltInRoleFilesTest` reads both defaults, and `OrbRulesTest` takes 20 units at level 100 for a file with 20. Fails if: a default differs, or the handler ignores the file. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-SOU-11 | When a bound Soul Collector works a pasture where orbs drop, its Experience Collector MUST fill with their XP. | MUST | R76, R79, ADR 0018 | The whole flow: orbs into the collector, then the button or a pipe to a player or a machine. | GameTest: a Gastly bound to a collector, and 10 orbs of 7 XP in the work area. Fails if: the collector holds less than 70 XP 400 ticks later. | target handler (XP) | agreed |
| CD-SOU-12 | For 6,000 ticks after a player dies, the Soul Collector handler MUST NOT take an orb within `player_distance` (default 8) blocks of the spot where that player died. | MUST NOT | R86, ADR 0018 | The coordinator: the orbs of a dead player were safe only until the player respawned, so another player's Ghost took them, against "never takes a player's XP" (R86). The spot of a death counts as a player who is no spectator (CD-SOU-05). An orb despawns 6,000 ticks after it drops, so the spot outlives the orbs of its death. The spots live in memory, at most 256, and an expired spot goes. | Unit: `OrbRulesTest`: an orb 7.9 blocks from a death spot stays, and an orb 8.1 blocks away is taken. `DeathSpotsTest`: a spot counts 5,999 ticks after the death and not 6,000 ticks after it, a spot of another level or outside the box counts not, and the 257th spot lets the oldest go. Inspect: `PlayerDeaths` records each death of a `ServerPlayer` from NeoForge's `LivingDeathEvent` that no listener canceled. Fails if: a result differs, or a death is not recorded. | target handler (XP) | agreed |

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-FRZ-01 | The role registry MUST enable the Freezer role, with the handler `cobblemon_dynamics:freezer`, for the Ice type, with its auto-assign flag on. | MUST | R76, R79, G13, ADR 0019 | The approver: "give ice the job to cool nearby water blocks", and the coordinator: "Automatic in Work Mode pastures". | Unit: `BuiltInRoleFilesTest` reads `freezer.json`: handler `cobblemon_dynamics:freezer`, types `ice`, `auto_assign` true, `quick_work` false, priority 0. Fails if: a value differs. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-02 | The work engine MUST NOT give a Quick Work order for a role whose role file sets `quick_work` to false. | MUST NOT | R76, R79, ADR 0019 | The approver: "no quick work". An auto-assign role takes Quick Work orders by CD-QWK-03, so the role file opts out. A file without the key keeps that rule. | Unit: `QuickWorkRulesTest`: a Freezer aimed at a water block gets no order, and a Stoker aimed at a furnace gets one. Fails if: the Freezer gets an order, or the Stoker does not. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-03 | The Freezer handler MUST match only a `minecraft:water` block at its source level whose cell above holds air, inside the work area. | MUST | R76, R79, RES13, ADR 0019 | The approver: "causing them to ice over on-top". Only the top layer of a lake freezes, and `matches` reads two cells for the block scan. | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: a source under air matches, and a source under a source, a flowing water cell, a waterlogged slab and a bubble column do not. Fails if: either result differs. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-04 | While a Freezer is Working, the handler MUST freeze one cell every ceil(1,200 ÷ ceil(F × L ÷ 100)) ticks, where F is the role file's `cells_per_minute_at_level_100` (default 60), rounded up to a whole number, and give the Freezer no demand while F is 0 or less. | MUST | R79, R84, BM8, ADR 0019, limits sweep | The coordinator asked for a pace. Level 1 freezes 1 cell per minute, level 50 freezes 30 and level 100 freezes 60. A cold biome tries about 75 columns per minute in each chunk (BM8). The review found that a pace of 0 froze 1 cell per minute, and that a fractional pace lost its fraction (R84). | Unit: `FreezePaceTest`: the interval is 1,200 ticks at level 1, 40 at level 50 and 20 at level 100. A file with 0 or -5 freezes nothing, and a file with 0.5 freezes 1 cell per minute at level 100. Fails if: an interval differs, a pace of 0 or less freezes, or a fraction is dropped. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-05 | Each freeze MUST turn into `minecraft:ice` the freezable cell nearest to the worker within `reach` (default 5.0) blocks, measured between block centres, and the claimed cell only when no other freezable cell lies in reach. | MUST | R76, R79, ADR 0019 | The approver: "in a 5block radius of the ice pokemon". The worker freezes outward from itself and leaves its claimed cell for last, so it clears its reach before it moves. | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: of cells at 2, 4 and 6 blocks with the claimed cell at 1, the freezes take 2, then 4, then the claimed cell, and never the cell at 6. Fails if: another order results, or a cell beyond 5 blocks freezes. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-06 | The Freezer handler MUST NOT freeze a water cell that has farmland within 4 blocks of it horizontally, at its height or one block below it. | MUST NOT | R79, RES13, ADR 0019, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "skip water that hydrates farmland (vanilla range)". `FarmBlock.isNearWater` checks the box from (−4, 0, −4) to (4, 1, 4) around the farmland (RES13). | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: a source 4 blocks from farmland at its height, and one 4 blocks from farmland one block lower, stay, and a source 5 blocks away freezes. Fails if: either result differs. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-07 | The Freezer handler MUST NOT freeze a `minecraft:bubble_column` block. | MUST NOT | R79, ADR 0019, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "skip the top of a bubble column". A frozen top would stop an elevator. CD-FRZ-03 already leaves the block out, and this row keeps the rule explicit. | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: the top block of a bubble column under air is not freezable. Fails if: it is. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-08 | The Freezer handler MUST NOT freeze a source whose horizontal neighbour or whose cell below holds flowing water. | MUST NOT | R79, ADR 0019, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "skip sources that feed a flow". A stream, an item channel or a waterfall keeps its source. | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: a source beside a flowing cell, and a source over a flowing cell, stay. Fails if: either freezes. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-09 | The Freezer handler MUST NOT freeze a cell whose block light is 10 or more. | MUST NOT | R79, RES13, ADR 0019 | Vanilla freezes no water there, and ice there melts again on its own random tick, so a Freezer would loop (RES13). | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: block light 9 freezes and 10 does not. Fails if: either result differs. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-10 | In the tick of each freeze, the Freezer handler MUST ask the use question and the break question of that cell, and freeze nothing there when one refuses. | MUST | R79, R73, ADR 0014, ADR 0019, misuse sweep | The coordinator: "Freezing is a block change, so it asks the claim mods' 'may break' check (FTB EDIT_BLOCK) for each cell." | GameTest: the test claim provider refuses one cell of a pond. Fails if: that cell freezes. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-11 | If a question of CD-FRZ-10 refuses, then the Freezer handler MUST treat that cell as not freezable for that worker for 6,000 ticks. | MUST | R79, ADR 0019, failure sweep | The worker does not ask again on every freeze. | Unit: a refused cell leaves the order of CD-FRZ-05 for 6,000 ticks. Fails if: it returns sooner. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-12 | When the claimed cell of an auto-assigned Freezer has no demand, the work engine MUST release the worker's claim within 20 ticks. | MUST | R79, R44, ADR 0019 | The coordinator: "It can walk on the new ice to reach further water." The next scan pass gives the nearest freezable cell, and the new ice gives it work spots, as the farming roles go crop to crop (CD-CRP-10). | GameTest: a level 100 Snom beside a pond of 9 × 9 water sources. Fails if: a source of the pond is still water after 6,000 ticks, or the Snom holds a claim on ice for more than 20 ticks. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-13 | The Freezer handler MUST read `cells_per_minute_at_level_100` (60) and `reach` (5.0) from its role file. | MUST | R79, ADR 0006 | Role numbers live in role files (CD-CFG-07). | Unit: `BuiltInRoleFilesTest` reads both defaults, and `FreezePaceTest` gives an interval of 40 ticks at level 100 for a file with 30. Fails if: a default differs, or the handler ignores the file. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-14 | The Freezer handler MUST make no sound: it posts no level event and plays no sound. | MUST | R76, CD-FX-09, RES13, ADR 0019 | All work is silent (CD-FX-09). | CD-FRZ-15 | The Freezer handler MUST NOT freeze a source when a block of `#cobblemon_dynamics:needs_adjacent_water` holds one of its four horizontal neighbours, the block above one of them, or the block below it. The tag holds sugar cane, `#minecraft:coral_blocks`, `#minecraft:corals`, `#minecraft:wall_corals`, the conduit and Create's encased fan as an optional entry. | MUST NOT | R84, ADR 0019, misuse sweep | The review found that farmland was the only block that kept its water. Sugar cane breaks at its next growth, coral blocks die, a conduit stops and Create's bulk washing stops when their water freezes. | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: a block of the tag at each of the 9 cells keeps the source, and a diagonal block, a block two away and a block under a neighbour do not. `FreezerTagFilesTest` reads the tag. Fails if: a result differs, or the tag lacks an entry. | target handler (water) | agreed |
| CD-FRZ-16 | The Freezer handler MUST NOT freeze a source while an entity other than a dropped item, an XP orb or a work focus entity is in the cell or in the water column under it, down to the first cell that is not water and at most 3 blocks. | MUST NOT | R84, ADR 0019, misuse sweep | The review found that a diver one block down, or a boat, could be sealed in under the ice. A dropped item or an orb needs no open water. The engine puts a work focus entity at the centre of a Working worker's target for its effect, so a guard that counted it would never let a Freezer freeze its own cell. | Unit: `FreezeRulesTest`: the occupant box reaches 3 blocks down a deep column and stops at the first cell under the source that is not water, and an occupied cell is not freezable. GameTest: `FreezerTests` ices over its pond with the work focus entity at each claimed cell. Fails if: a result differs, or a claimed cell stays water. | target handler (water) | agreed |

A full plant of height 18 uses about 1,870 J per tick (BM9), so no role file passes it, and negative heat would cool the target (R85). | Unit: `HeaterMathTest`: level 1 gives 15, level 50 gives 750 and level 100 gives 1,500, and a role file with 10,000 gives 1,870 at level 100 and one with −50 gives 0. Demo: check 65. Fails if: a value differs, or the Demo plant does not warm. | compat | agreed |
| CD-HTR-05 | The Mekanism Heater handler MUST report demand only while its target's heat handler has a capacitor whose temperature is below 3,000 K. | MUST | RES10, ADR 0020 | An unformed plant has no capacitor, and 3,000 K is the plant's own cap on its speed (RES10). | Unit: `HeaterMathTest`: no capacitor and 3,000 K give no demand, and 2,999 K gives demand. Fails if: either result differs. | compat | agreed |
| CD-HTR-06 | Where PneumaticCraft: Repressurized is loaded, the role registry MUST enable the Heater role `cobblemon_dynamics:heater_pneumaticcraft` for the Fire type, with its auto-assign flag off, for every block entity with `PNCCapabilities.HEAT_EXCHANGER_BLOCK` on a horizontal face. | MUST | R80, RES12, ADR 0020 | Ruling 3 of R80: "PneumaticCraft heat (its public API)". Fails if: a value differs, or the role is not active with PneumaticCraft. | role registry | agreed |

Fails if: a value differs, or the compressor's temperature does not rise. | compat | agreed |

| CD-FRM-08 | Where a mod of the crop table of ADR-0020 is loaded, the farming roles MUST grow and harvest each of its families as the table states, and leave the growth-only look-alikes of R90 to the Cultivator alone. | MUST | R80, R90, RES11, ADR 0020 | As CD-CRP-18 for the families of 1.1. The COHO families get a Demo on the COHO server. A pick by the loot of a growth-only look-alike drops nothing or the plant (R90). | Analysis: the case `mods` gives each family its adapter in the farming lines (CD-CRP-05). Demo: check 66 on the COHO server: one crop of each COHO family of the table, grown and harvested once. Fails if: a family differs from the table, no worker works it, or a Harvester takes a growth-only look-alike. | target handler (farming) | agreed |

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
