A mixin on its heat level holds it, as the Blaze Burner mixin does (CD-SUP-01, CD-BRN-01), and its own fuel stays. | GameTest: in the compat run, a level 50 Dragonair at a liquid burner under a basin. Fails if: the burner does not read `SEETHING`, or its own fuel changes. | compat | agreed |
| CD-MCH-02 | The role registry MUST enable the Crystal Tender for the blocks of `#cobblemon_dynamics:crystal_tender_targets`, whose default holds `#c:budding_blocks`, Cobblemon's nine budding tumblestones, Unusual End's `budding_citrine` and the unwaxed corundum of Quark. | MUST | R80, RES11, RES12, ADR 0020 | Ruling 1 of R80. Quark's corundum and Unusual End's citrine are in no `c` tag, and a tag of our own keeps the `c` tags as their mods wrote them. | Unit: `CrystalTagFilesTest`: the tag holds each default, and each entry of another mod is optional. Fails if: one is missing or required. | build | agreed |
| CD-MCH-03 | The Crystal Tender handler MUST report demand on a block of `#cobblemon_dynamics:crystal/self_growing` only while Cobblemon's own rule lets it grow: a block of `#cobblemon:tumblestone_heat_source` within 1 block, and a next stage. | MUST | R80, RES11, RES13, ADR 0020 | A budding tumblestone grows by itself into its next stage, and only beside lava or a magma block (RES11, RES13). | Unit: `BuddingRulesTest`: a small budding tumblestone beside lava has demand, and one with no heat source has none. GameTest: a Geodude at a small budding tumblestone beside a magma block. Fails if: a result differs, or the tumblestone does not grow within 3,000 ticks. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-04 | The Crystal Tender handler MUST give a block of `#cobblemon_dynamics:crystal/scheduled_tick` its scheduled `tick` in place of a random tick, on 1 extra tick in `scheduled_tick_one_in` (default 5), count only air as an open face of such a block, and count the blocks of `#cobblemon_dynamics:crystal/buds` as buds. | MUST | R80, R88, RES11, ADR 0020 | Unusual End's budding citrine grows only under a worker: its random tick does nothing in Minecraft 1.21.1, and nothing else gives it its scheduled tick. That tick grows a random face into air with no roll of its own, so the gate of 1 in 5 gives it the pace of budding amethyst, whose random tick grows a face 1 time in 5 (R88). Its buds are in no `c` tag (RES11). | Unit: `BuddingRulesTest`: a citrine bud on a face gives demand, a water source on a face is no open face, and the extra tick calls `tick` on 1 roll in 5 and nothing on the other 4, with `scheduled_tick_one_in` 5 in the default file. Demo: check 66. Fails if: a result differs. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-05 | If 200 extra ticks in a row change no block of a Crystal Tender's target and of its faces, then the handler MUST report no demand on that target to that worker for 6,000 ticks. | MUST | R80, RES12, ADR 0020, failure sweep | Some budding blocks grow only in some places, such as Just Dire Things' time crystal outside its dimension or Quark's corundum above y 24. A budding block with one free face grows on a tick about 1 time in 30, so 200 misses in a row happen about 1 time in 900. | Unit: `CrystalStallTest`: 199 unchanged ticks keep the demand, the 200th removes it, a changed block resets the count, and the demand returns 6,000 ticks after the stop. Fails if: the stop comes earlier or later, or never ends. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-06 | Where Create: Enchantment Industry and Apothic Enchanting are loaded, the Muse role MUST work CEI's Infuser while the Infuser stands over its basin, and add its Quanta of CD-MUS-07 to the stats that the Infuser gathers at the position below it. | MUST | R80, R87, RES11, ADR 0020 | The audit: the Infuser gathers the table stats of the block below it, which the existing stats mixin already changes, and it needs a target and a demand rule. CEI gathers the stats only while the Infuser is idle, so the demand is the assembled Infuser, not a running infusion (R87). Extra Quanta can push a recipe out of its stat window, and the Muse is Work Leash only, so the binding is the player's opt-in. | Unit: `CeiInfuserRuleTest`: the stats one block below the Infuser and the basin two below, and the demand with the basin and none without it. `MuseStationsTest`: a gather below a station finds the station above it. `MuseBonusTest`: the Muse takes the Infuser only with Apothic Enchanting, with 45 Quanta at level 100, and the tag holds it as optional. Demo: check 66. Fails if: a result differs, or the Demo infusion reads no extra Quanta. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-07 | Where Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks is loaded, while a Brewer works an Alchemist Cauldron, the handler MUST advance each running cook time by one extra step per tick, never past its end. | MUST | R80, RES11, ADR 0020 | The audit's row: 100 ticks per input item, behind the private `cooktimes`. The cauldron then finishes each item itself, as the Brewer leaves the last step of a brew to vanilla (CD-BRW-02). | GameTest: in the compat run, a Grimer at a cauldron with one input. Fails if: the item takes more than 55 ticks, or a cook time passes its end. | compat | agreed |
| CD-MCH-08 | While a Generator works a Cobblemon Healing Machine that holds less than its maximum charge, the handler MUST add charge at G × L ÷ 100 times the machine's own recharge rate through `setHealingCharge`, where G is the role file's `recharge_multiplier_at_level_100` (default 4). | MUST | R80, RES11, BM13, ADR 0020 | The audit: "cap at a multiple of Cobblemon's own recharge". Cobblemon recharges `maxHealerCharge` in `secondsToChargeHealingMachine` (BM13). | Unit: `HealingChargeTest`: with 6 charges in 900 s, level 100 adds 4 × 6 ÷ 18,000 per tick, level 50 adds half that, and a full machine gets nothing. GameTest: a level 100 Pikachu at an empty Healing Machine. Fails if: a rate differs, or the machine passes its maximum. | target handler (Cobblemon) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-09 | While a Pump is bound to farmland, the handler MUST keep each farmland block of the work area within 4 blocks of it horizontally, at its height, at moisture 7, report demand only while one of them is below 7 and the use question did not refuse it at the last check, count that demand at most once in 20 ticks, and ask the use question again for each refused block at each count. | MUST | R80, R90, RES11, ADR 0020 | The audit's row. One water source hydrates a 9 × 9 area of farmland, and the Pump stands in for it. Each changed block asks the use question in its tick. A Pump whose only dry farmland lies in a claim that refuses it would otherwise Work forever and spend its Work Stamina, and a count on each tick reads 81 blocks (R90). | Unit: `FarmlandMoistureTest`: farmland 4 blocks away reads 7 after a check, and farmland 5 blocks away or one block lower stays. A refused block gives no demand, is asked again at each count, and gives demand at the first count that allows it, and a count reads the area at most once in 20 ticks. GameTest: a Squirtle bound to farmland with no water near it, for 2,400 ticks. Fails if: farmland in range dries, farmland out of range changes, a refused block gives demand, or a count comes sooner than 20 ticks after the last count or check. | target handler (vanilla) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-10 | Telekinesis and the Magnet MUST reach a block entity that has no item capability and is a vanilla `Container` through `InvWrapper`, or through `SidedInvWrapper` for a `WorldlyContainer`, and take no stack that the container's `canTakeItem` refuses. | MUST | R80, R90, RES11, ADR 0020 | The audit: Cobblemon, CobbleFurnies, Handcrafted and Valhelsia Furniture register no item capability, so the logistics roles missed their containers. A hopper reaches them in the same way, and asks `canTakeItem` before each take, which neither wrapper asks. Cobblemon's TM Machine keeps its inputs that way, and Visual Workbench its crafting grid (R90). | Unit: `ItemPortsTest`: a test `Container` with no capability gives a port. GameTest: a Magnemite bound to Cobblemon's Gilded Chest collects a dropped item, and the fallback takes nothing from a slot that `canTakeItem` refuses, of a plain and of a sided container. Fails if: the chest gets no item, or a refused slot gives an item. | target handler (NeoForge caps) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-11 | Telekinesis and the Magnet MUST NOT reach a block of `#cobblemon_dynamics:logistics/no_fallback` through the fallback of CD-MCH-10, whose default holds Lootr's containers and Legendary Monuments' pedestals. | MUST NOT | R80, RES11, ADR 0020, misuse sweep | Lootr gives each player their own loot, and the pedestals are quest altars, not storage (RES11). Their ids are unverified, so the entries are optional. | Unit: a tagged `Container` gives no port. Fails if: it gives one. | target handler (NeoForge caps) | agreed |
| CD-MCH-12 | `docs/modpack-integration.md` SHOULD list the machines of other mods that the roles reach with no code, as the section "What works already" of ADR-0020 lists them. | SHOULD | R80, RES12, ADR 0020 | Pack makers learn that these machines need nothing. Acceptable to shorten the list to the mods of the COHO pack. | Inspect: the page lists them. Fails if: the list is missing. | build | agreed |

### BAL: balance

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-BAL-01 | With the default role files, the Generator's maximum output per worker MUST stay below 15,000 FE/t. | MUST | R25, BM3, G10 | 15,000 FE/t is the approver's figure for a small Extreme Reactors reactor, and one Cobblemon must not replace it. | Analysis: the highest default output is 2 × 5,000 = 10,000 FE/t, for a Lv100 legendary. Fails if: any default role file allows 15,000 FE/t or more per worker. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-02 | With the default config and role files, a full pasture of Lv100 Generators that are not legendary or mythical MUST average at most 40,000 FE/t over a work-and-rest cycle with no beds and no healers. | MUST | R25, G10 | A fleet can compete, at the cost of many Cobblemon and planned rest. | Analysis: 16 workers × 5,000 FE/t × 50% uptime = 40,000 FE/t. Fails if: the computed average is above 40,000 FE/t. | stamina | agreed |
| CD-BAL-03 | With the default config, the stamina component MUST NOT let a worker that no healer serves work more than 8 ÷ 9 (88.9%) of the time. | MUST NOT | R25, R11, G10 | Rest is the cost of every worker. | Analysis: uptime = 1 ÷ (1 + 1 ÷ f), and the best bed factor f is 8. Unit: a simulated Lv50 worker on a Starlit Bed over 10 cycles works 88.9% ± 0.1% of the time. Fails if: the uptime is above 89.0%. | stamina | agreed |
| CD-BAL-04 | The Wheel Driver, Cranker, Tailwind and Fan Catalyst handlers MUST NOT push a machine past Create's own maximum for that machine. | MUST NOT | R25, RES2, RES5 | These roles are exempt from the output limits because the machine caps their output. | GameTest: a worked small wheel gives at most 8 RPM, a large wheel 4 RPM, a hand crank 32 RPM and a windmill 16 RPM, and a worked fan keeps Create's processing time. Fails if: any machine exceeds its Create maximum. | target handler (Create) | agreed |
| CD-BAL-05 | With the default role files, one Telekinesis or Magnet worker MUST move at most 64 items per second. | MUST | R25, R27, R31 | Item transfer stays at the approved rates. | Analysis: ceil(64 × 100 ÷ 100) = 64. Fails if: a default role file allows more than 64 items per second per worker. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-06 | Dynamics MUST NOT raise Cobblemon's limit of 16 Cobblemon per pasture. | MUST NOT | R25, RES1 | The pasture cap limits each fleet. | GameTest: with the default Cobblemon config, try to pasture a 17th Cobblemon in a pasture in Work Mode. Fails if: Cobblemon accepts it. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-BAL-07 | With the default role files, the Crystal Tender MUST NOT give a budding block more extra random ticks than one AE2 Growth Accelerator gives. | MUST NOT | R25, RES3 | A powered accelerator array stays the better choice. | Analysis: 1 extra random tick per 10 ticks equals AE2's default accelerator interval. Fails if: a default role file sets a shorter interval. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-08 | With the default role files, one Cultivator MUST apply at most 30 growth uses per minute. | MUST | R25, R72, R75, BM5, G10, ADR 0010 | A player who holds bone meal applies 300 uses per minute (BM5), so a level 100 Cultivator does a tenth of that. It uses no bone meal, and each use costs Work Stamina (CD-CUL-06). | Analysis: ceil(30 × 100 ÷ 100) = 30 uses per minute at level 100, the highest level. Unit: `BalanceFilesTest` reads 30 from the default `cultivator.json`. Fails if: a default role file allows more than 30 uses per minute. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-09 | With the default role files, one Harvester MUST harvest at most one crop per 20 ticks. | MUST | R25, R72, BM4, G10, ADR 0010 | A Mechanical Harvester of Create takes every fully grown crop that its contraption passes (BM4), so engineering harvests a field far faster than one Cobblemon. A Harvester takes one crop per claim and walks between crops. | Analysis: one harvest per claim, `harvest_ticks` (20) after the work starts. Unit: `BalanceFilesTest` reads 20 from the default `harvester.json`. Fails if: a default role file allows a harvest sooner than 20 ticks after the work starts. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-10 | With the default role file, one Lumberjack MUST fell at most 4 logs per second of Working. | MUST | R25, R79, R81, BM6, G10, ADR 0010 | A player with an iron axe breaks a log in 10 ticks, and Oritech's Treefeller fells 20 logs per second (BM6). A level 100 Lumberjack, the fastest, fells 4 logs per second and also waits for each regrowth. | Analysis: `ticks_per_log_at_level_100` 5 gives 20 ÷ 5 = 4 logs per second. Unit: `BalanceFilesTest` reads 5. Fails if: a default role file allows more. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-11 | With the default role file, one Soul Collector MUST take at most 10 orb units per second. | MUST | R25, R79, BM7, G10, ADR 0010 | A player takes one orb unit per 2 ticks (BM7). The role makes no XP, so the farm's own output caps it. | Analysis: ceil(10 × 100 ÷ 100) = 10. Unit: `BalanceFilesTest` reads 10. Fails if: a default role file allows more. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-12 | With the default role file, one Freezer MUST freeze at most 60 cells per minute. | MUST | R25, R79, BM8, G10, ADR 0010 | A cold biome tries about 75 columns per minute in each chunk (BM8). | Analysis: ceil(60 × 100 ÷ 100) = 60. Unit: `BalanceFilesTest` reads 60. Fails if: a default role file allows more. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-13 | With the default role files, one Heater MUST give at most 1,500 J per tick to a Mekanism block and at most the heat of one lava block to a PneumaticCraft block. | MUST | R25, R80, BM9, BM10, G10, ADR 0010 | M6: "capped below what a full plant can use", about 1,870 J/t (BM9). A level 100 Generator through a Resistive Heater gives 7,500 J/t, so the Heater stays below the Generator. | Analysis: ceil(1,500 × 100 ÷ 100) = 1,500, and the PneumaticCraft form never passes one lava block at 1,300 K. Unit: `BalanceFilesTest` reads both files. Fails if: a default role file allows more. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-14 | The handlers of CD-AIR-02, CD-AIR-05 and CD-KIN-02 MUST NOT push a machine past its own maximum: `windGenerationMax` for the Wind Generator, the thunderstorm factor for the IE Windmill, and an optimized overshot wheel for the IE Watermill. | MUST NOT | R25, R80, BM11, BM12, ADR 0010 | Machine-capped roles are exempt from the output limits, as CD-BAL-04 states for the machines of Create. | Analysis: the formulas of the three rows reach the maximum at level 100 and never pass it. Unit: `WindBonusTest` and `IeWindmillMathTest`. Fails if: a value passes its maximum. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-15 | With the default role files, a Stoker of 1.2 MUST give each machine of another mod at most the free fuel of CD-FUEL-01 to CD-FUEL-12, at that machine's own speed, one machine per worker. | MUST | R25, R80, ADR 0010 | ADR-0010: "The Stoker and the Brew Stoker give free fuel only, at vanilla speed, one machine per worker." A modded machine's own speed stands in for vanilla speed (R80), and the gates and caps of CD-FUEL-02, CD-FUEL-04, CD-FUEL-05, CD-FUEL-08, CD-FUEL-11 and CD-FUEL-12 hold. | Analysis: each rule test of the FUEL rows. Fails if: a default role file allows more. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-16 | With the default role file, one Generator MUST recharge a Healing Machine at no more than 4 times the machine's own recharge rate. | MUST | R25, R80, BM13, ADR 0010 | The audit: "cap at a multiple of Cobblemon's own recharge". | Analysis: 4 × 100 ÷ 100 = 4. Fails if: a default role file allows more. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-BAL-17 | The 1.2 gate report MUST record the two balance flags of RES12 that the approver accepts: the Seething heat of a level 50 Superheater in PneumaticCraft machines, and the SU of a level 100 Generator through the motors of Create: New Age. | MUST | R25, R78, R80, RES12, ADR 0010 | The approver: "I do not mind if things are a bit unbalanced between the mods". Both work today with no code. | Inspect: `docs/testing/v1.2-gate.md` names both flags. Fails if: a flag is missing. | build | agreed |

Fails if: Cobblebase teleports it into water or freezes it. | compat | agreed |
| CD-CMP-04 | The compat component MUST NOT change Cobblebase's behaviour for pastures that are not in Work Mode. | MUST NOT | R20 | Other pastures keep their jobs. | Demo: a Cobblebase job in a pasture that is off. Fails if: the job stops. | compat | agreed |
| CD-CMP-05 | If the Cobblebase mixin fails to apply, then the compat component MUST log one warning and let the game continue. | MUST | RES4, failure sweep | Cobblebase broke on Cobblemon 1.8 once already. | Demo: run with a Cobblebase version that lacks the target method. Fails if: the game crashes, or the warning repeats. | compat | agreed |
| CD-CMP-06 | Where Cobbleworkers is not loaded, the compat component MUST load no Cobbleworkers compat class. | MUST | failure sweep, REV1 | A class that refers to a missing mod crashes the game. | GameTest: a server with Cobblebase and without Cobbleworkers starts. Fails if: an error names a Cobbleworkers compat class. | compat | agreed |
| CD-CMP-07 | Where Cobblebase is not loaded, the compat component MUST load no Cobblebase compat class. | MUST | failure sweep, REV1 | A class that refers to a missing mod crashes the game. | GameTest: a server with Cobbleworkers and without Cobblebase starts. Fails if: an error names a Cobblebase compat class. | compat | agreed |
| CD-CMP-08 | When mod loading completes, Dynamics MUST log one INFO line that names each mixin of its config for that side as applied, skipped with its reason, applied with no injection, or not applied yet. | MUST | R65, quality sweep, R71 | A pack maintainer checks the integration in the server log (R65). A mixin that skips because its mod is absent logged nothing before this row, so a log could not tell 4 skipped mixins from 4 missing ones. | Unit: the line for 8 applied mixins, 4 skipped because `cobblebase` is absent, 1 skipped because its target lacks a member, 1 with no injection and 2 not applied yet names each mixin under its heading. GameTest: the log of the test pack's `smoke` server holds the line once, with the 3 vanilla mixins and the 5 Create mixins applied, the 4 Cobblebase mixins skipped because `cobblebase` is absent, and the stats hook of CD-MUS-07 skipped because `apothic_enchanting` is absent. Fails if: a mixin is missing or under the wrong heading, or the line appears more than once. | compat | agreed |
| CD-CMP-09 | When the mod registers its network payloads, Dynamics MUST log one INFO line with the payload version and the id of each payload, by direction. | MUST | R65, quality sweep | A pack maintainer compares the payloads with the Network payloads table of `docs/modpack-integration.md` (R65). A client must have the same payload version as the server. | Unit: the line names `pasture_panel_state` and `leash_overview` to the client, and `work_mode_toggle`, `pasture_panel_open`, `pasture_panel_close` and `leash_overview_request` to the server, with version 3. GameTest: the log of the test pack's `smoke` server holds the line once. Fails if: a payload or the version is missing, a direction is wrong, or the line appears more than once. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-CMP-10 | When the role registry loads the role files, or a config change changes the active roles, Dynamics MUST log one INFO line with the number of roles loaded, the number active, and each disabled role with its reason. | MUST | R65, quality sweep | A pack maintainer sees at once which roles the server runs (R65). The lines of CD-CFG-10 name one disabled role each and give no total. | Unit: a build of the 16 role files without the 5 Create handlers, with 1 more role in `roles.disabled`, logs "16 loaded, 8 active, 8 disabled" with each disabled id and its reason, and a rebuild with no change logs nothing. GameTest: the log of the test pack's `smoke` server holds "16 loaded, 16 active, 0 disabled" once, and the log of the `without-create-ae2` case names the 7 Create roles as disabled. Fails if: a count, a disabled id or a reason is missing, or a rebuild with no change logs the line. | role registry | agreed |

### SEC: misuse

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-SEC-01 | If a player who is neither the placer nor an operator sends a Work Mode request, then the server MUST reject it and leave Work Mode unchanged. | MUST | R24, misuse sweep, actor × action sweep | Strangers must not stop a base. | GameTest: a player who is not the owner requests off. Fails if: Work Mode changes. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-SEC-02 | If a pasture has no recorded placer, then the server MUST accept Work Mode requests from operators only. | MUST | R39, misuse sweep | An ownerless pasture has no placer to trust. | GameTest: clear the pasture's `ownerId`, then a regular player requests on, then an operator does. Fails if: the regular request changes Work Mode, or the operator's request does not. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-SEC-03 | If a player sends a Work Mode request within 10 ticks of that player's last accepted request, then the server MUST ignore it. | MUST | R24, misuse sweep, limits sweep | Request spam must not flood the server. | Unit: a request 9 ticks after the last accepted one is ignored, and one 10 ticks after is applied. Fails if: either result is wrong. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-SEC-04 | If the requesting player is more than 10 blocks from the pasture or in another dimension, then the server MUST reject the Work Mode request. | MUST | RES1, misuse sweep, limits sweep | Cobblemon links a player to a pasture only within 10 blocks. | GameTest: requests from 10.0 blocks, from 10.1 blocks and from the Nether. Fails if: the 10.0 request fails, or another request succeeds. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-SEC-05 | The Work Mode payload MUST carry the requested state, so a repeated payload gives the same result. | MUST | R50, failure sweep | A duplicated packet must not flip the setting back. | Unit: two identical "on" payloads 10 ticks apart. Fails if: Work Mode reads off. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-SEC-06 | If two Work Mode requests for one pasture arrive in the same tick, then the server MUST apply them in arrival order and send every viewer the final state. | MUST | failure sweep | Two operators must see the same result. | Unit: on, then off, in one tick from two operators. Fails if: the final state is on, or a viewer shows on. | pasture adapter | agreed |
| CD-SEC-07 | If a player uses the Work Leash on a Cobblemon that the player does not own, then the server MUST refuse the selection. | MUST | R3, misuse sweep | Players must not command other players' Cobblemon or wild Cobblemon. | GameTest: player B uses the leash on player A's pastured Cobblemon and on a wild Cobblemon. Fails if: either Cobblemon is selected. | content | agreed |
| CD-SEC-08 | The server MUST evaluate every bind rule from its own state. | MUST | misuse sweep | A modified client must not bind outside the rules. | Unit: a bind call that names a Cobblemon with no server-side selection on the player's leash. Fails if: the bind succeeds. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-SEC-09 | If a player below permission level 2 runs a `/cobblemondynamics` command, then the server MUST refuse it. | MUST | R39, misuse sweep | The claim list names other players' Cobblemon. | GameTest: a level-0 player runs both commands. Fails if: either command runs. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-SEC-10 | If a player sneak-right-clicks a wheel, a crank or a fan with the Work Leash while that target is not driven, or while the player is not its binder, its placer or an operator, then the server MUST leave its direction and mode unchanged. | MUST | REV1, misuse sweep, actor × action sweep | A stranger must not reverse a build, and Create destroys a generator that turns against its network. | GameTest: a second player clicks a worked wheel, a worked crank and a worked fan, and then the binder clicks a wheel that no worker drives. Fails if: any direction or mode changes. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-SEC-11 | If another mod cancels the player's right-click on a block, then the Work Leash MUST NOT bind that block. | MUST NOT | RES3, misuse sweep | Protection mods cancel right-clicks on blocks that the player may not touch, so the leash obeys them. | GameTest: an event listener cancels right-clicks on a chest, and the player clicks the chest with a selected Psychic-type. Fails if: a pending source or a binding exists. | content | agreed |

### PERF: performance

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-PERF-01 | While 64 workers are Working, Dynamics MUST use at most 1.0 ms of server time per tick on average. | MUST | R24, quality sweep | A large base must not lag the server. | Analysis: a spark profile of the reference test world (8 pastures, 64 Working workers) over 6,000 ticks. Fails if: the mean Dynamics time is above 1.0 ms per tick. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-PERF-02 | While 64 workers are Working, Dynamics MUST keep its 95th-percentile server time at or under 2.0 ms per tick. | MUST | R24, quality sweep | Spikes lag players even when the mean is low. | Analysis: the spark run of CD-PERF-01. Fails if: the p95 is above 2.0 ms per tick. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-PERF-03 | If no worker of a pasture is eligible for the Crystal Tender role, then the work engine MUST NOT iterate block positions in that pasture's scan. | MUST NOT | R24, quality sweep | Machines and beds are block entities, which each chunk lists. | Unit: a scan with only Fire-type workers. Fails if: the scan reads one block state or more. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-PERF-04 | The effects component MUST keep at most one work focus entity for each claim. | MUST | quality sweep | Leaked anchors cost memory and bandwidth. | GameTest: create and release 10 claims, 5 times. Fails if: the focus entity count ever exceeds the claim count. | effects | agreed |
| CD-PERF-05 | Dynamics MUST NOT log at INFO level or above from per-tick code. | MUST NOT | quality sweep | Log spam hides real errors. | Analysis: run the reference test world for 6,000 ticks and count Dynamics log lines after startup. Fails if: a line is not a once-per-cause warning. | work engine | agreed |

### CFG: configuration and datapacks

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-CFG-01 | Dynamics MUST read its server settings from the NeoForge server config `cobblemon_dynamics-server.toml`. | MUST | R9 | Server owners tune balance without datapacks. | Inspect: after the first start of a new server, its `config/` folder holds the file with every key in this section. Fails if: the file or a key is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-02 | The server config MUST accept a horizontal and a vertical work radius from 2 to 32 blocks each, with defaults of 8 and 6. | MUST | R24, REV1, limits sweep | 32 is Cobblemon's default roam limit. | Unit: for each radius, 2 and 32 are accepted, and 1 and 33 are rejected. The defaults read 8 and 6. Fails if: any result is wrong. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-03 | The Generator role file MUST hold the FE rate at level 1 (default 10), the FE rate at level 100 (default 5,000), the rarity multiplier (default 2) and the rarity labels (default `legendary` and `mythical`). | MUST | R5, REV1, ADR 0006 | Role-specific numbers live in the role file, so packs tune power output with a datapack. | GameTest: a datapack sets the level-100 rate to 2,500, then `/reload`. Fails if: a Lv100 Generator does not give 2,500 FE/t. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CFG-04 | The server config MUST hold the work capacity per level (default 3,600 ticks), the four bed factors (defaults 2, 3, 4 and 8) and the healer recovery times at level 1 and level 100 (defaults 1,200 and 120 min). | MUST | R10, R11, R14 | Packs tune stamina balance. | Unit: set the capacity to 7,200 ticks per level. Fails if: a Lv1 worker is Tired before 7,200 ticks of work, or is not Tired after 7,201 ticks. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-05 | The server config MUST let operators disable each role by its id. | MUST | R9 | A pack drops a role that clashes with its balance. | GameTest: disable the Generator, then an Electric-type stands beside a battery for 400 ticks. Fails if: it claims the battery. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CFG-06 | The client config MUST hold no work sound option, because work effects play no sound (CD-FX-09). | MUST | R21 | R21 made work sounds a client choice. The approver's play-test ruling (2026-11-10) makes all work silent, so the option would turn on nothing. | Unit: the client config has no `effects.workSounds` key. Fails if: the key exists. | client | agreed |
| CD-CFG-07 | The role registry MUST load each role from `data/{namespace}/cobblemon_dynamics/roles/{role}.json`, with its eligible types, auto-assign flag, Quick Work flag, priority, range, handler, handler parameters and effect. | MUST | R24, REV1, ADR 0006, R70 | Pack makers add or change roles without code. Role files hold no species lists. | GameTest: a datapack lets Water-types act as Stokers. Fails if: a Water-type does not claim a furnace after `/reload`. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CFG-08 | When an operator runs `/reload`, the role registry MUST apply changed role files without a restart. | MUST | R24, ADR 0006 | Pack makers test changes in one session. | GameTest: change the Stoker's types, then run `/reload`. Fails if: the old types still apply. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CFG-09 | If a role file fails to parse, then the role registry MUST skip that file and log one error that names it. | MUST | failure sweep, ADR 0006 | One bad file must not break every role. | GameTest: load a datapack with one malformed role file. Fails if: the game crashes, another role stops, or the error lacks the file name. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CFG-10 | If a role file names a handler that is not registered, then the role registry MUST disable that role and log one line that names it. | MUST | R9, failure sweep, ADR 0006 | Create roles must switch off when Create is absent. | GameTest: start a server without Create. Fails if: a Create role is active, or the log shows more than one line per disabled role. | role registry | agreed |
| CD-CFG-11 | The server config MUST hold the scan interval (default 40 ticks) and the block-check budget per pasture per tick (default 512). | MUST | R24, REV1 | Server owners trade scan speed for server time. | Unit: set the interval to 80 and the budget to 256. Fails if: passes start more often than every 80 ticks, or a tick checks more than 256 positions. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-12 | The server config MUST hold the travel timeout (default 600 ticks) and the wait after a missed arrival (default 6,000 ticks). | MUST | R24, REV1 | Packs with slow pathing need longer timeouts. | Unit: set the timeout to 1,200 ticks. Fails if: an auto-assigned worker's work claim on an unreachable target is not held at tick 1,199 after the claim, or is still held at tick 1,201. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-13 | The work engine MUST NOT cap a role's output below the values in its role file. | MUST NOT | R5, REV1 | A pack that raises a rate gets that rate, and no hidden cap overrides the role file. | GameTest: a datapack sets the Generator's level-100 rate to 20,000. Fails if: a Lv100 legendary Generator gives less than 40,000 FE/t. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CFG-14 | The work engine MUST clamp each configured work radius to Cobblemon's `pastureMaxWanderDistance`. | MUST | RES1, REV1, limits sweep | Cobblemon teleports or recalls a pastured Cobblemon that leaves its roam box. | Unit: with `pastureMaxWanderDistance` set to 20, a configured radius of 32 acts as 20, and a configured radius of 19 stays 19. Fails if: either result is wrong. | work engine | agreed |
| CD-CFG-15 | The server config MUST hold the Standby move delay, with a default of 200 ticks. | MUST | R44 | Packs tune how long a worker waits before it moves. | Unit: set the delay to 400 ticks. Fails if: an auto-assigned worker moves after 399 ticks in Standby, or stays after a scan at 400 ticks. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-16 | The server config MUST hold `quickWork.enabled`, with a default of `true`. While it is `false`, the work engine traces no aim and starts no Quick Work order. | MUST | R63, R9, ADR 0013 | A server whose protection mod only cancels clicks turns Quick Work off, because a ray-traced target makes no click (ADR 0013). | Unit: the key exists with the default `true`, and the trace range rule gives no trace while the value is `false`. Fails if: either check fails. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-17 | The server config MUST accept a Quick Work target range, `quickWork.targetRange`, from 2 to 24 blocks, with a default of 16. | MUST | R63 | Ruling 1 sets 16 blocks. The trace never reaches past the leash range of CD-CFG-18. | Unit: 2 and 24 are accepted, 1 and 25 are rejected, and the default reads 16. Fails if: any result is wrong. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-18 | The server config MUST accept a Quick Work leash range, `quickWork.leashRange`, from 2 to 24 blocks, with a default of 16. The trace of CD-QWK-01 reaches the smaller of the two ranges. | MUST | R63, ADR 0013, limits sweep | Ruling 4 sets 16 blocks. A worker within 6 blocks of a target at 24 blocks stays inside the teleport distance of 32 blocks that Cobblemon gives party Cobblemon. A trace that stops at the leash never starts an order beyond it. | Unit: 2 and 24 are accepted, 1 and 25 are rejected, the default reads 16, and a target range of 24 with a leash range of 8 traces 8 blocks. Fails if: any result is wrong. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-19 | The server config MUST accept `logistics.maxWorkersPerContainer` from 1 to 16, with a default of 5. | MUST | R66, R9, limits sweep | The coordinator's ruling: 5 places per container are enough that a container rarely runs out, and a server owner changes the cap here. | Unit: 1 and 16 are accepted, 0 and 17 are rejected, and the default reads 5. Fails if: any result is wrong. | build | agreed |
| CD-CFG-20 | The server config MUST accept `experienceCollector.capacity` from 1,000 to 1,000,000, with a default of 100,000. | MUST | R79, ADR 0018, limits sweep | The coordinator: "Capacity: propose one, for example 100,000 XP, as a config key." 1,000,000 XP at 20 mB per XP stays within the `int` of a fluid stack. | Unit: 1,000 and 1,000,000 are accepted, 999 and 1,000,001 are rejected, and the default reads 100,000. Fails if: any result is wrong. | build | agreed |

### ART: art (Phase 2)

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-ART-01 | Dynamics MUST ship, for each block that it adds (the four beds and the Charge Cell), an original block model, original block textures and an item model that renders that block model, so that the item shows the placed block. | MUST | R32, R60, G11 | The beds and the Charge Cell are new blocks, and their art represents the 3D object that a player places (approver's art ruling, 2026-11-10). | Inspect: the jar holds a block model, its textures and an item model whose parent is that block model, for each bed and the Charge Cell. Fails if: an asset is missing, or a block's item model is a flat sprite. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-02 | Each block's art MUST represent its placed 3D object. The beds keep the models and textures of the Phase 1 build. | MUST | R32, R33 | The approver ruled on 2026-11-10 that block art can represent the 3D object, as the beds do, and that only items that are never placed need new pixel art. | Demo: the approver reviews each block on the review spritesheet. Fails if: the approver rejects a block. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-03 | Dynamics MUST ship an original Work Leash item texture that shows a lead with a red-and-white clasp. | MUST | R32, R33 | The leash is a new item. | Demo: the approver reviews the texture on the review spritesheet. Fails if: the texture is missing, or the approver rejects it. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-04 | Dynamics MUST ship original UI sprites for the Work Mode button in 4 states (on, off, disabled, hover), the Work Stamina bar, and the 7 state icons. | MUST | R32, R33, R38, G7 | The panel and the head bars need their own sprites. | Inspect: the jar holds 4 button sprites, the bar sprites and 7 icon sprites. Demo: the approver reviews them on the review spritesheet. Fails if: a sprite is missing, or the approver rejects one. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-05 | Dynamics MUST ship the approver's logo, scaled from `art/logo/logo-source-1254.png`, as a `logo.png` of 256 × 256 pixels and as a project-page icon of 512 × 512 pixels. | MUST | R32, R53 | The approver supplied the logo, and the mod list and both release pages show it. | Inspect: the jar's `logo.png` is 256 × 256 pixels and matches `art/logo/logo-256.png`, and `art/logo/icon-512.png` is 512 × 512 pixels. Fails if: a size differs, a file is missing, or the jar's logo differs from `art/logo/logo-256.png`. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-06 | Every block and item texture MUST be 16 × 16 pixels. | MUST | R32, limits sweep | The approver chose vanilla 16 × 16 style. | Inspect: a script reads every PNG under `assets/cobblemon_dynamics/textures/block/` and `assets/cobblemon_dynamics/textures/item/`. Fails if: a texture is not 16 × 16, for example 17 × 16 or 32 × 32. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-07 | Every block model MUST be a Minecraft JSON block model that Blockbench opens. | MUST | R32 | Contributors edit the models in Blockbench. | Inspect: each model loads in the game with no model warning. Demo: each model opens in Blockbench. Fails if: either check fails for a model. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-08 | The repository MUST keep the source file of every asset under `art/`. | MUST | R32 | A contributor must be able to edit the art from its source. | Inspect: each shipped texture and model has a source file under `art/`. Fails if: a source file is missing. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-09 | The jar and the repository MUST NOT contain pixels copied from Mojang, Cobblemon, Create or AE2 assets. | MUST NOT | R32, RES1, RES2, RES3 | Cobblemon models carry a NonCommercial license and Create assets are All Rights Reserved. | Analysis: compare each texture with the textures of Minecraft 1.21.1, Cobblemon 1.8.4, Create 6.0.10 and AE2 19.2.19, tile by tile at 16 × 16. Fails if: any tile matches exactly. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-10 | Before the public release, the approver MUST approve each asset on the review spritesheet. | MUST | R32 | The approver decides how the mod looks. | Inspect: the review record holds an approval for every asset in CD-ART-01 to CD-ART-05. Fails if: an asset lacks an approval. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-11 | Phase 1 and Phase 2 builds MAY ship placeholder art. | MAY | R32, R33 | Testing does not wait for the art. When a private build has no placeholders, it ships the final art that exists. | Inspect: a Phase 1 build with a placeholder bed texture passes CI. Fails if: CI rejects a private build for placeholder art. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-12 | The public release MUST NOT ship placeholder art. Block art that represents its placed 3D object (CD-ART-02) is release art, not placeholder art. | MUST NOT | R32 | Public players get the approved art. | Inspect: every texture in the 1.0.0 jar matches its approved file under `art/`. Fails if: a texture ships that the review record does not approve at its current source. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-13 | Every asset MUST carry the MPL-2.0 license. | MUST | R8, R32 | One license covers the code and the art. | Inspect: the repository `LICENSE` covers `art/` and `assets/`, and no asset file states another license. Fails if: an asset states another license. | content | agreed |
| CD-ART-14 | The mod metadata MUST set `logoBlur=false`. | MUST | R53 | The logo is pixel art, and blur smears it when the mod list scales it. | Inspect: `neoforge.mods.toml` in the jar holds `logoBlur=false`. Fails if: the key is missing or true. | build | agreed |

### REL: CI, release and docs

| ID | Requirement | Level | Source | Rationale | Acceptance test | Owner | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CD-REL-01 | The repository MUST run the build, the unit tests and the GameTests in GitHub Actions on every push and pull request to `main`. | MUST | R9, R24 | Every change is tested before it merges. | Demo: open a pull request with a failing unit test. Fails if: its check passes. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-02 | The repository MUST run CodeQL and dependency review from `.github/workflows/security.yml`. | MUST | R24 | Public code gets security scans on every change. | Inspect: the workflow runs both jobs. Fails if: either job is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-03 | The repository MUST keep a `.github/dependabot.yml` for the Gradle and GitHub Actions ecosystems. | MUST | R24 | Dependencies stay current. | Inspect: the file lists both ecosystems. Fails if: either is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-04 | When a maintainer pushes a `v*` tag with no pre-release suffix, the release workflow MUST attach the jar to a GitHub release that is not a pre-release, and publish the jar to each of Modrinth and CurseForge whose token and project id the repository holds. | MUST | R9, G9, R56, R69 | The approver ruled a public release. The approver uploads the jar to CurseForge by hand: "why does it need project ids and api tokens? I already released it with the beta.2 build and it just required the JAR". By the coordinator's ruling (R69), a site without its settings gets a notice instead of a failed job, and the upload stays automatic for a site whose settings exist. | Inspect: `ReleaseWorkflowTest` reads `release.yml`. The settings check of the publish job sets one output for each site, writes a `::notice::` that names each absent setting, and never exits non-zero. The publish step of each site runs only when its token and project id are present. Demo: push the tag `v1.0.0` with no publishing settings. Fails if: the workflow fails, the GitHub release lacks `cobblemon_dynamics-1.0.0+mc1.21.1.jar` or is a pre-release, the run shows no notice, or a site whose settings are present lacks version `1.0.0+mc1.21.1` after the workflow ends. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-05 | The build MUST version releases as `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH+mc1.21.1`. | MUST | R24 | Players see the game version in the version name. | Inspect: the mod version in the v1.0.0 jar reads `1.0.0+mc1.21.1`. Fails if: it has another form. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-06 | The repository MUST license its code under MPL-2.0. | MUST | R8, G9 | The approver chose MPL-2.0. | Inspect: `LICENSE` holds MPL-2.0, and `neoforge.mods.toml` declares `license = "MPL-2.0"`. Fails if: either differs. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-07 | The jar MUST NOT contain copies of Cobblemon assets. | MUST NOT | RES1, misuse sweep | Cobblemon models carry a NonCommercial license, so the mod refers to them by id. | Inspect: list the jar. Fails if: a file sits under `assets/cobblemon/`. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-08 | The repository MUST hold a player guide at `docs/player-guide.md` that covers Work Mode, the Work Leash, Quick Work, Work Stamina, beds, healers, the Charge Cell and every role. | MUST | R9, R49, R60, R63 | Public players need a guide. | Inspect: at the v1.0.0 tag, the guide has a section for each item. Fails if: a section is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-09 | The repository MUST hold a manual checklist at `docs/testing/manual-checklist.md` that lists every `Demo:` test in this sheet. | MUST | R24, R49 | Demo tests need a repeatable run. | Inspect: at the v1.0.0 tag, compare the checklist with the Demo tests in this sheet. Fails if: a Demo test is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-10 | The GameTest run MUST load Cobblemon 1.8.4, Create 6.0.10 and AE2 19.2.19. | MUST | R24, RES3 | Integration tests need the real mods. | Inspect: the CI log of a GameTest run lists the three mods at these versions. Fails if: a mod is missing or at another version. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-11 | The README SHOULD state that Cobblemon Dynamics is not affiliated with Cobblemon, The Pokémon Company, Create or Applied Energistics 2. | SHOULD | R9 | A clear notice protects a fan project. Acceptable to put the notice on the Modrinth and CurseForge pages instead. | Inspect: the README and both release pages. Fails if: none of them holds the notice. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-12 | When a maintainer pushes a `v*` tag with a pre-release suffix, the release workflow MUST NOT publish to Modrinth or CurseForge. | MUST NOT | R16, R56 | Phase 1 and Phase 2 builds are private tests. | Demo: push `v1.0.0-alpha.1`, then `v1.0.0-beta.1`. Fails if: either version appears on either site. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-13 | The project MUST NOT merge Phase 2 work before every Phase 1 row at level `MUST` or `MUST NOT` passes on a named commit and the approver has play-tested a Phase 1 build. | MUST NOT | R16, R33 | The approver ruled a v1 test before v2. | Inspect: the M5 milestone records a passing run on a named commit and the approver's play-test note, both dated before the first M6 merge. Fails if: either record is missing, or is dated after that merge. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-14 | The project MUST NOT publish 1.0.0 before every row at level `MUST` or `MUST NOT` in this sheet passes on a named commit and the approver has play-tested a Phase 2 build. | MUST NOT | R16, R33 | The approver ruled a v2 test before the public release. | Inspect: the M8 milestone records a passing run on the release commit and the approver's play-test note, both dated before the `v1.0.0` tag. Fails if: either record is missing, or is dated after the tag. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-15 | When 1.0.0 is published, the approver's modpack MUST include it. | MUST | R16, G9 | The approver ruled that Phase 3 ends with the mod in the pack. | Inspect: the pack's mod list at its next release names Cobblemon Dynamics 1.0.0. Fails if: the mod is missing from that release. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-16 | The project MUST NOT publish 1.1.0 before every row of 1.1 at level `MUST` or `MUST NOT` passes on a named commit, every earlier row at those levels still passes there, and the approver has play-tested a 1.1 build. | MUST NOT | R72, R73, R16, CD-REL-14 | COHO ships the public 1.0.0, so a 1.1.0 that breaks a 1.0 role reaches its players at once. The approver tests on the COHO server, so the play-test also runs the Demo of CD-CRP-18. | Inspect: the 1.1 gate report, `docs/testing/v1.1-gate.md`, records a passing result of every `MUST` and `MUST NOT` row on the release commit, and the approver's play-test note. Fails if: a row has no passing result there, or the note is missing. | build | agreed |
| CD-REL-17 | The project MUST NOT publish 1.2.0 before every row of 1.2 at level `MUST` or `MUST NOT` passes on a named commit, every earlier row at those levels still passes there, the test-pack case `mods` passes, and the approver has play-tested a 1.2 build. | MUST NOT | R76, R77, R80, CD-REL-16 | COHO ships the public release, so a 1.2.0 that breaks a role of 1.1 reaches its players at once. The approver tests on the COHO server, so the play-test also runs the Demos of 1.2. | Inspect: `docs/testing/v1.2-gate.md` records a passing result of every such row on the release commit, the result of the case `mods` and the approver's play-test note. ## Open questions

The approver answered Q1-Q8 on 2026-11-09, Q9 on 2026-11-11, and Q10 and Q11 of 1.1 on 2026-11-12 (R75). The
coordinator ruled on the other design decisions of the [1.1 plan](2026-11-12-v1.1-farming.md#design-decisions-for-the-approver)
on 2026-11-12 (R74). The coordinator ruled on Q12 to Q20 and on the other design decisions of the
[1.2 plan](2026-11-15-v1.2-new-roles-and-compat.md#design-decisions) on 2026-11-15 (R81), under the approver's
delegation. Q12 to Q20 set how the game plays, so the approver's go on the plan summary confirms them.

- Q9 (answered on 2026-11-11 by R67): must the workers of a Work Mode pasture ask claim mods? Yes. The approver approved
  claim checks for Work Mode pastures (R67): the auto-assignment and the workers of a pasture never work a target in a
  chunk that the pasture's owner may not interact with. The check works from the owner's UUID, so an offline owner
  counts. The LND rows hold the answer.
- Q10 (answered on 2026-11-12 by R75): may a Ground-type grow apricorns? No. The approver: "no". The default tag of the
  Cultivator leaves them out, so apricorns keep their natural pace, and a pack can add them. CD-CUL-10 and CD-CRP-01 hold
  the answer.
- Q11 (answered on 2026-11-12 by R75): are the pace and the cost of the Cultivator right? Yes. The approver: "accept".
  ceil(30 × L ÷ 100) growth uses per minute, and 100 ticks of Work Stamina per use beyond the drain of Working. A level
  100 Cultivator then applies 30 uses per minute, a tenth of a player who holds bone meal, and works about 22% of the time
  with no bed and about 70% on a Starlit Bed. CD-CUL-04, CD-CUL-06, CD-CUL-08 and CD-BAL-08 hold the answer.

- Q12 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81, decision 1 of the 1.2 plan): is the Lumberjack's pace right: 10 ticks of Working per
  log at any level, with Work Stamina only through Working? No. The pace grows with level, from 20 ticks per log at level 1
  to 5 at level 100, and Work Stamina drains only through Working. CD-LUM-11, CD-LUM-26 and CD-BAL-10 hold the ruling.
- Q13 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 2): are the Lumberjack's defaults right: a cap of 1,024 leaves, mangroves skipped, four saplings or one
  for a 2 × 2 trunk, the shell felled with the tree, and trees with a bee nest skipped? CD-LUM-05, CD-LUM-08, CD-LUM-09 and
  CD-LUM-18 hold the defaults.
- Q14 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 3): are the Soul Collector's pace and claims right: ceil(10 × L ÷ 100) orb units per second, and up to
  5 workers on one collector? CD-SOU-03, CD-SOU-06 and CD-BAL-11 hold the defaults.
- Q15 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 4): are the Experience Collector's capacity of 100,000 XP and its recipe right? CD-XPC-14 and CD-CFG-20
  hold the defaults.
- Q16 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 5): is the Freezer's pace right: ceil(60 × L ÷ 100) cells per minute, with no cell at block light 10 or
  more? CD-FRZ-04, CD-FRZ-09 and CD-BAL-12 hold the defaults.
- Q17 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 6): is the Heater right: 1,500 J/t at level 100 on Mekanism, the heat of one lava block on
  PneumaticCraft, and leash only on PneumaticCraft? CD-HTR-04, CD-HTR-06, CD-HTR-07 and CD-BAL-13 hold the defaults.
- Q18 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 7): is the Egg Incubator of Cobblemon Incubator a Stoker target, which the plan builds, or an animal row
  of R80, which ruling 1 cuts? CD-FUEL-11 holds the default.
- Q19 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 8): do the fixes ship with 1.2.0, or first as a patch 1.1.1? The FIX rows hold the default: 1.2.0.
- Q20 (ruled on 2026-11-15 by R81: the default stands. Decision 9): do the move effects stand: Aerial Ace for the Lumberjack, Night Shade for the Soul Collector and Ice
  Beam for the Freezer? The Role table holds the defaults.
